# UI/UX & Accessibility Review Registry

**What this is.** The append-only, repo-side record of UI/UX, accessibility (WCAG), code-review, and orchestration (how-we-worked) findings on PoeTech surfaces — surfaced INSIDE the app (Projects › Build › Quality / Proof, Governor-gated) so the reviews close their own loop where the work lives, not only in session notes. Since DR-0102 the panel also MEASURES this registry's freshness (days since the newest record) and flips to attention past 7 days — consistency is the schema, freshness is measured, not promised.

**Why it exists.** Darrell, 2026-06-16: *"our app UI/UX reviews — are they in there?"* They lived in CI, review docs, and the local-LLM output, not in the app. This is the structured, real feed the in-app panel reads (parsed at build time into `__UIUX_REVIEWS__`, the same pattern as the governance queue and the DR ledger). The live local-LLM diff review and the live WCAG contrast measurement render alongside these records from their own real sources.

**Binding rule — no fabricated reviews.** Every record below points at a real artifact in this repo (a source doc, a gate script, or a DR). `Status: addressed` is used ONLY where the resolution is independently verifiable (a passing gate, an injected build marker). Otherwise the status is `logged` (recorded, resolution not verified here) or `open` (known, unresolved). Add a record when a review happens; never invent one to make the panel look green.

**Format (parsed):** one record per `###` block. Fields: `Date`, `Surface`, `Type` (`accessibility` | `ui-ux` | `security` | `code-review` | `orchestration`), `Status` (`addressed` | `open` | `logged`), `Findings`, `Source`. An `orchestration` record reviews how the work itself ran (lanes, verification, integration, handoffs) per DR-0102 — one per reviewed working day, frictions carried as actions with re-review dates.

---

## Records

### REV-0174 · UI/UX — dark-theme theme-marker chips unreadable (Jdg invisible) + FEEDBACK pill occluding the reader toggle
- **Date:** 2026-07-23
- **Surface:** app/src/components/BibleReader.jsx · app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (feedback FAB) · app/src/lib/scripture-highlights.js (root cause)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Darrell 2026-07-23 (screenshot, Church → Scripture on the dark theme: "UIUX reviews?"). Two defects: (1) the **Judgment (Jdg)** theme chip was invisible and **Sin** nearly so — their palette styles are ink-only reader marks for parchment (Box ink `#1A1815`, Struck ink `#4A4640`) rendered raw on the dark surface; the styles with their own light backgrounds (Grace/Love/Spirit/…) read fine, which localized the fault to ink-only styles. Fix: `markerChipCss` — the abbr marker always sits on a parchment plate (`#FAF8F4`) when its style has no background of its own, so every marker reads "as it looks on the page" on ANY surface, both render sites. (2) The **💬 Feedback pill** (fixed bottom-left) sat over the reader's Plain/Patterns/Voices toggle at that scroll position, occluding a tappable control. Fix: compact-when-idle — un-revealed it shrinks to a 48px circle (smallest WCAG tap target) and expands to the labeled pill on reveal/hover/focus, cutting the idle occlusion footprint ~70%. Residual: any fixed FAB can still transiently cover scrolling content at one scroll position — accepted for now with the compact footprint; **re-review: 2026-08-18** alongside the DR-0224 anxiety-clarity gate pass (candidate: FAB auto-dodge or drag-to-move like the video dock).
- **Source:** app/src/__tests__/bible-reader-render.test.jsx

### REV-0173 · Fix + Way — merge duplicates: floating Combine bar (no jump) + full text on selected rows; "Inline, No Jumping"
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/components/Imported.jsx · docs/decisions/DR-0201-*.md · docs/00-foundations/_root/UX-PATTERNS.md · app/src/__tests__/imported-render.test.jsx
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Darrell 2026-07-20 (screenshot: two duplicate Jul-01 payroll rows checked at the BOTTOM of the register, but the "Combine 2" bar sat at the TOP — forcing an eye-jump; and descriptions truncated so PPD IDs couldn't be verified). The combine LOGIC was already comprehensive (keeps the fullest row, warns if amounts differ). Fixes: (1) the combine action bar is now a FLOATING bar (fixed, bottom, above the corner FABs) so it's always in view while checking rows anywhere; (2) a SELECTED row un-truncates to full text (whitespace-normal break-words) so the family can verify the full PPD ID before merging — and the tap-to-expand detail already shows "Full description". Declared the Way (DR-0201): "Inline, No Jumping" — an action/change appears where the eye already is, never a jump to another screen region, never off-screen; the one allowed movement is an explicit "take me there" via smooth scroll. Added to UX-PATTERNS.md (Design Principle 6). Noted the KPI-View scroll for re-review against the Way (it's an explicit go-there, so it stands). Proven-to-catch: imported-render (+1: combine bar has `fixed`, selected row un-truncated, combine keeps fullest). Lint + consistency + full suite green.
- **Source:** app/src/__tests__/imported-render.test.jsx

### REV-0164 · Extend — L46: the non-father's takeaway + what the father takes from the watchers
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js (L46 benefits/lesson/quiz/facilitator extended) · docs/decisions/DR-0194-*.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Extend-a-lesson (DR-0168 harvest lineage), declared by Darrell 2026-07-20 ("How should other who are not fathers understand the Will of Yahweh for them... and what should the father take away from the watchers?"). Built two reciprocal takeaways into L46: (a) NON-FATHERS carry the same verb with wider hands — "provoke... unto love and to good works" is to the whole Body (Heb 10:24), spiritual parenthood is real without a child (1 Cor 4:15; 2 Tim 1:5; Titus 2:3-4), all father the fatherless (Jas 1:27; Ps 68:5-6), the childless bear eternal fruit (Isa 56:4-5); (b) THE FATHER takes humility FROM the watchers — he is a watched, fathered SON not a lone patriarch (Ecc 4:9-12; Heb 12:9; Heb 5:8), the Body's correction is grace not surveillance (Prov 27:6,17), even his children disciple him (Matt 21:16). The full circle: watchers become active spiritual parents; the father becomes a humble son. Every verse KJV-verbatim from public/bible/kjv (DR-0076); lint + lesson-flow + research-integrity + presentable green.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0194-the-non-father-takeaway-and-the-father-and-the-watchers.md

### REV-0163 · Harvest — Living Lesson L46: "Ye Fathers — Provoke to Good Works, Not to Wrath" (spoken teaching + verified neuroscience)
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js (L46 new module + meta.weeks 45→46) · docs/decisions/DR-0193-*.md · docs/99-session-notes/2026-07-20-ye-fathers-provoke-to-good-works.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Harvest-to-lesson (DR-0168) of Darrell's spoken teaching 2026-07-20 ("Bible says to the fathers to provoke their children to good works not wrath and why explicitly fathers... neuroscience"). Built as L46: the TWO provocations (forbidden to wrath Eph 6:4/Col 3:21, commanded "to provoke unto love and to good works" Heb 10:24); WHY "ye fathers" — first image of God the Father (Eph 3:15), the strength that builds/crushes "lest they be discouraged" (Col 3:21), the covenant hinge (Deut 6:6-7/Mal 4:6), the template of God's discipline (Heb 12:9); the active father PRAYS/TEACHES/TRAINS/PROVOKES (Job 1:5/Deut 6:7/Prov 22:6/3 John 1:4); comfort for the fatherless (Ps 68:5/Luke 11:13). NEUROSCIENCE verified BEFORE ship (the DR-0192 rule, first applied here from the start), tiered per DR-0100: TIER A — fatherhood lowers testosterone (Gettler PNAS 2011), involvement blesses child (Sarkadi 2008) real-but-modest (McLanahan 2013), paternal HARSHNESS most tied to child depression/low-worth (Kane & Garber 2004, the lab's witness to Col 3:21); TIER B — paternal brain (Feldman 2014), activation relationship (Paquette), rough-and-tumble play (StGeorge & Freeman 2017); TIER C REFUSED — the viral fatherless-home stats (untraceable, compiler conceded faulty) kept OUT, and "father shapes child's God-image" held as theology not science. Audience text attributed, no bare stat. Surfaces the FATHERS-PROVOKE-TO-GOOD-WORKS.md foundation (DR-0094 pair). Every verse KJV-verbatim from public/bible/kjv (DR-0076); lint + lesson-flow + research-integrity + presentable green.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0193-ye-fathers-provoke-to-good-works-lesson.md

### REV-0162 · Trust but Verify — retroactive audit of the 8 research-sourced lessons (independent, primary-source)
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js (L15, L34, L37, L38, L40 corrected; L36, L39, L41 verified clean) · docs/decisions/DR-0192-*.md · memory/MEMORY.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** How-we-worked review (DR-0108), declared by Darrell 2026-07-20 ("Apply the trust-but-verify pass to the other research-sourced lessons") — executing the retroactive lens DR-0190 named. Ran 8 INDEPENDENT parallel verifiers (one per lesson), each extracting every external claim and checking it against PRIMARY sources via live web research (DR-0076 §7 independent method), tiering per DR-0100. Clean: L36 (Halwell real/attributed), L39 (LXX/MT/Cainan/Ebionites/Amarna/Sennacherib/lions all accurate, fringe framed as refuted), L41 (Huff+Bartlett verified, apologetics framed as TIER-B witness, lament-fraction accurate). Corrected: **L15** — Brooks's TALK "L" is LEVITY not Listening (a named framework taught wrong, with a whole pillar built on it) + the "Get Excited" study domain is a timed MATH task not negotiation; **L40** — Sargon's annals confirm the conquest/27,290/resettlement "in the midst of Assyria" but NOT the Halah/Habor/Gozan/Medes addresses (Scripture's alone) — the "consistent-with = confirmed" over-reach, fixed in 8 spots; **L38** — a communication adage was mis-credited to Golden (traces to W.H. Whyte, Fortune 1950), reattributed; **L34** — "forgoing" → "willing to forgo" ~$2M equity (precision); **L37** — ACTN3 "<1%" sharpened to "sprint-performance variance." PROCESS LESSON (the durable output): Darrell's mid-session "did separate research occur?" showed the DR-0190 pass had run only when challenged; L15/L40 prove that's insufficient — the pass is now a PRE-SHIP gate on any research-sourced lesson, not a when-asked step. New verses (Prov 17:22, Eccl 3:4) fetched verbatim from public/bible/kjv (DR-0076); lint + lesson-flow + research-integrity + presentable gates green.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0192-retroactive-trust-but-verify-audit-of-research-sourced-lessons.md

### REV-0161 · Harvest — Living Lesson L45: recognition, credit, and equity meet the Word ("honour to whom honour")
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js (L45 new module + meta.weeks 44→45 + cross-refs into L12/L42/L44) · docs/decisions/DR-0191-*.md · docs/99-session-notes/2026-07-20-recognition-power-equity-lesson.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Harvest-to-lesson (DR-0168) + trust-but-verify (DR-0190), declared by Darrell 2026-07-20 ("Lesson for Ari and claude... review our Ways and documentation and create a new lesson and add this context to those that it will help add quality"). Source: the IT-workplace session "Recognition, Power, and Equity in IT Work" (Courtney Fleeger & Mark McCarthy) — credit FUNCTIONS AS POWER; uneven recognition sorts who advances and falls hardest on the overlooked across identities and career stages. Built as L45 "Give Honour Where It Is Due" on the tested module shape: the Word founded EQUITY deeper — God gives "more abundant honour to that part which lacked" (1 Cor 12:23-26); honour is a DUE (Rom 13:7; Prov 3:27; Jas 5:4); rest for the unthanked (Heb 6:10); guardrail against a credit-grab (Matt 6:1; Prov 27:2; John 5:44); no partiality (Jas 2:1-9); the servant-king (Mark 10:43-45; Luke 14:10-11); no reward stolen — God pays by labour (1 Cor 3:6-8; Gal 6:4). Bound as a CONDUCT STANDARD for Ari/Claude (render honour to whom honour, name the quiet contributor, never absorb the family's credit — GOVERNANCE-EXECUTION-ADVISORY/DR-0111). **Ways-review question answered honestly mid-session (Darrell: "did separate research occur like the last documents?"):** the first pass did only ONE verification search; on the challenge the agent ran the full DR-0190 pass and traced PRIMARY sources — Sarsons, *American Economic Review* 2017 107(5):141-45 (+ *JPE* 2021 129(1):101-147) and Ross et al., "Women are credited less in science than men," *Nature* 2022 (PMC9352587; ~13% attribution gap across almost all career stages) — folded into the lesson comment + DR-0191, audience text kept attributed-general with no bare stat. LESSON FOR THE WAYS: the DR-0190 separate-research pass is not optional on a research-sourced harvest and must run BEFORE first ship, not only when challenged. Every verse KJV-verbatim from public/bible/kjv (DR-0076); lint + lesson-flow + research-integrity + presentable gates green.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0191-recognition-and-equity-honour-to-whom-honour.md

### REV-0160 · Trust but Verify — external research is independently checked before the platform teaches it
- **Date:** 2026-07-20
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js (L44 Safe to Speak + the trust-but-verify harvest note) · app/src/__tests__/living-lessons-research-integrity.test.js · docs/decisions/DR-0190-*.md · memory/MEMORY.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** How-we-worked review (DR-0108), declared by Darrell 2026-07-20 ("do our own separate research on the cited research... trust but verify... add to our Ways and implement"). Friction/gap: a lesson was built from a forwarded newsletter, and without a standard the agent could have taught the newsletter's claims (incl. a cluster of circulated %-stats) on the platform's own authority. Action taken (not a note): the agent independently verified the primary sources — Project Aristotle (2012, 180+ teams) + Edmondson (1999) as established fact; the 381-employee AI/depression study as REAL (Kim/Kim/Lee, Humanities & Social Sciences Communications, Nature 2025) with an ethical-leadership buffer the newsletter under-played; the circulated %-stats as un-traceable and therefore NOT asserted. Recorded the standard as DR-0190, added the memory `feedback_trust_but_verify_external_research`, and shipped the proven-to-catch gate (a lesson stating a statistic without an attributing source fails the build). Re-review: retroactively apply the tier lens to the other research-sourced lessons (L15/L34/L36/L38/L41) on the standing cadence.
- **Source:** app/src/__tests__/living-lessons-research-integrity.test.js

### REV-0006 · Orchestration review — the 2026-07-05 working day
- **Date:** 2026-07-05
- **Surface:** How the work itself ran (PRs #585–#587) — lanes, verification, integration order, handoffs
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Kept: six parallel audit lanes over all 39 surfaces with adversarial file:line classification (DR-0076); proven-to-catch tests on all four financial-math fixes; every non-live item carries its why + re-review date (DR-0075); small follow-up lanes (#586/#587) integrated cleanly. Frictions → actions: (1) one 59-file PR carried five workstreams — discovery may batch, but its fixes integrate as separate small lanes per DR-0077 (practice, effective now, re-review 2026-07-12); (2) the 0077 migration unlock is still a hand-paste for Darrell while the db-migrate lane exists — name the cause (NAS family instance vs cloud secret) and close it (re-review 2026-07-12); (3) this registry sat 20 days without a record across ~200 merges — freshness is now measured in-app and orchestration reviews append per working day (DR-0102). **(4) THE STALL — "we don't move when I'm not pushing" (Darrell, remedy asap):** verified root cause — the auto-open-PR + auto-merge lane filtered eligible branches to `^(feat|fix|merge|docs)/`, so every `claude/*` agent PR (all remote-session work, incl. this one) was invisible to the hands-off merge lane and could only be landed by Darrell's hand. Fixed this session: `claude/**` added to the CI push trigger, auto-open-PR trigger, and auto-merge eligibility, so agent PRs ride the existing default (auto-merge on green gates; `hold` label = the per-PR soak/Governor brake). Behavioral half: idle turns now pull the next dated backlog item forward instead of parking on passive poll-timers (recorded to memory). Re-review 2026-07-12.

### REV-0007 · Inbound (Voice Ops) — opportunities & constraints
- **Date:** 2026-07-05
- **Surface:** Inbound tab (app/src/components/Inbound.jsx) + backend/voice-worker + the sibling infra/nas-property-inbound pipeline
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Reality-traced evaluation of our inbound capability. WHAT IT IS: Twilio-transcribed voicemails on two business lines (Steward Real Estate, Cornerstone Tech) → a Cloudflare Worker (free tier) + D1 → in-app manual triage into Incident / Inquiry / Project; the TLC clinical line is deliberately NOT routed (HIPAA, Phase 3). A second, separate inbound pipeline (infra/nas-property-inbound) ingests tenant TEXTS as classified DATA. OPPORTUNITIES (ranked): (1) auto-suggest triage — the worker has zero intent logic; port the NAS deterministic classifier (and later a DR-0105 sovereign-model summary) to pre-fill intent/priority/unit/target [ADDRESSED THIS SESSION, deterministic half — lib/inbound-triage.js]; (2) unify the two inbound pipelines onto the one CRM backbone (crm_leads, migration 0046, crm_capture_lead seam) — one governed front door (re-review 2026-08-15); (3) the missing outbound half — a human-approve-to-send reply path in-app (both systems are capture-only by design) (re-review 2026-08-15); (4) more channels (SMS/web/email) onto the same queue (re-review 2026-09-01); (5) COLG/church inbound reuse of the same front door (re-review 2026-09-01). CONSTRAINTS: HIPAA firewall is a hard line (established); per-device localStorage config (no synced steward config); test-coverage gap — pure convert logic is tested but the React fetch/mark-handled path + worker endpoints are not (this surface already shipped a real double-convert bug, A2); free-tier Twilio/Cloudflare ceilings (tracked via /usage/this-month); any added automation/model is Tier-C three-brakes (ships inert). All capability surfaces IN the app; only the webhook receiver, ingest loops, and model host live outside (they can't run in a browser) and each surfaces + is governed in-app (DR-0061/0065).
- **Source:** app/src/lib/inbound-triage.js

### REV-0008 · YouTube transcription / Harvest Ledger — opportunities & constraints
- **Date:** 2026-07-06
- **Surface:** Harvest Ledger (Church -> Harvest; app/src/components/HarvestLedger.jsx) + the transcript pipeline (infra/nas-sme-pipeline/load-transcripts.py, ops-runner.py, transcript-backfill-ci.py, .github/workflows/transcript-backfill.yml) + the pure harvest core (app/src/lib/video-harvest.js, transcript-harvest.js)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Reality-traced review of how we turn a service video into content. HOW IT WORKS: one-source-many-harvests (DR from 2026-06-25 note) -- an ingested @thelovecorner recording (choir_sermons row) fans into 9 harvest types; "now" types (Message/Scripture-from-title/Songs/Events) light the instant a row lands from real fields, "caption" types (Transcript + Lessons/Discernment/Testimony/Trivia, and the full Scripture sweep) mine from the video's YouTube AUTO-CAPTIONS (no GPU) via youtube-transcript-api, upserted into video_transcripts (0058) and recomputed live by video-harvest.js; Whisper-on-NAS (large-v3-turbo, port 8771) is the RARE no-caption fallback. THREE FETCH PATHS, one queue: the steward taps "Fetch next transcripts"/"Resume after YouTube block" in-app -> a row in ops_commands (0068, owner/admin RLS) -> the NAS ops-runner.py (outbound poll, service key, whitelist, three brakes, SHIPS INACTIVE per P10-12) runs load-transcripts.py; plus a workflow_dispatch CI backfill and a local standalone script -- the cloud app can't reach the LAN NAS (P18), so they meet in the DB (DR-0088). Coverage is DERIVED, never painted (DR-0076): the header today reads 135 ingested, 0 fully mined, 135 partly (avg ~41%), Transcript 57/135 captioned. OPPORTUNITIES (ranked): (1) VERIFY-THE-CLIMB -- no synthetic asserts the Harvest % actually moved after a "successful" run; add a proven-to-catch gate so a green run that advanced 0 while gaps remain fails (closes the exact P22 false-green; re-review 2026-07-13); (2) ARM THE NAS RUNNER + a residential proxy (Webshare/YT_PROXY_URL) as the one reliable no-block route, then finish the last 78 captions -- this is the single unblock that climbs the % (re-review 2026-07-13); (3) AUTO-DETECT new uploads -- the hourly schedule ships commented-out in transcript-backfill.yml; arm it (idempotent, cheap) so today's Sunday/Wednesday self-ingests within the hour (re-review 2026-07-20); (4) DEEPEN partial->complete -- the caption extractors are honest heuristics (partial); a later sovereign-LLM pass over the SAME transcript deepens Lessons/Discernment/Testimony to complete without re-fetch (Tier-C, three-brakes; re-review 2026-08-15); (5) WIRE the last-mile consumers -- Trivia (already text, sequenced first), Lessons->Learn, Discernment track, Sermon Stories; each caption harvest should light its destination surface, not just the ledger (re-review 2026-08-15); (6) coverage display beyond YouTube -- buildLedger joins choir_sermons (YouTube-only), so the RSS/other adapters (SOURCE-ADAPTER-INTERFACE) won't show until the join reads content_sources per-platform (re-review 2026-09-01). CONSTRAINTS: YouTube IP-blocks datacenter ranges (GitHub runners always; the NAS residential IP after ~180 req/day) -- the hard ceiling on throughput (LESSONS P22/P23, 2026-07-02 incident); classify-before-persist is load-bearing (a RUNNER failure writes NOTHING, only an ITEM verdict is stored, or a transient block becomes permanent false state); the NAS runner + hourly schedule ship INACTIVE and are Tier-C three-brakes; NAS-side multi-harvest extraction is a Darrell's-hand deploy, not yet verified live; no YouTube cookies used (proxy + jittered sleeps + kill-switch/resume is the whole anti-block strategy). All controls surface IN the app; only the caption fetcher, ops-runner, and Whisper host live outside (they can't run in a browser) and each is queued + reported live in-app (DR-0088/0061).
- **Source:** app/src/components/HarvestLedger.jsx

### REV-0009 · Ways-review — the ConnectBot miss (scope to the team's tools, not the agent's)
- **Date:** 2026-07-06
- **Surface:** How the work ran this session — the agent's scoping of a NAS action; the SSH-access assumption
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** First ways-review under DR-0108 (Darrell: "review our ways I have connectbot... Make that a documented mandatory thing"). THE MISS: on opportunity #1 (finish the captions) the agent said the NAS route "needs your hand / I have no SSH" and stopped -- scoping the fix to its OWN access -- while Darrell has ConnectBot (SSH from his phone), so the NAS runbook was executable by the principal the whole time. Root cause: the agent treated its own verified limit (no ssh client; LAN + Tailscale Funnel blocked from the cloud -- that part WAS measured, a DR-0076 win) as the TEAM's limit, and treated "must be by hand" as a stopping point instead of an unverified premise. KEPT (evidence): the SSH claim was verified before repeating, not asserted from memory (no ssh binary, LAN:22 unreachable, Funnel 403); opportunity #1 was driven as far as the cloud allowed (dispatched transcript-backfill.yml, read the real log: 0 fetched / 14 blocked / exit 3, classify-before-persist held -- no corpus poisoning); the DR-0107 deploy gap was caught and the deploy dispatched + verified on the merge SHA. FRICTIONS -> actions: (1) the agent must inventory the whole team's capabilities (Darrell's tools, the Foundation's, the NAS's) before declaring a path blocked -- encoded as WAYS-REVIEW + the Layer-0 "Review Our Ways" rule; ConnectBot recorded as a known capability so NAS runbooks ship as paste-ready SSH steps, not "unreachable" (effective now); (2) a stated "can't / must be by hand" is challenged as a premise, not accepted (SURFACE-PREMISE); (3) ways-reviews now append here per DR-0102 cadence so the practice is perpetual, not remembered. Honest limit (DR-0076): this is conversational scoping behavior -- no build gate can catch it; the always-loaded Layer-0 rule IS the control.
- **Source:** CLAUDE.md

### REV-0010 · Ways-review — the self-imposed arming gate (over-applied a safety rule; friction landed on Darrell)
- **Date:** 2026-07-06
- **Surface:** The deploy-freshness safeguard's arming posture; the agent's repeated "you do it" asks
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Second ways-review under DR-0108, triggered by Darrell: "connectbot? have you reviewed our ways?" THE MISS: the agent built the durable deploy-healer (DR-0109) but GATED it behind a manual `DEPLOY_FRESHNESS_ENABLED='true'` arm citing P11 ("ship inert"), then pushed the arming ask at Darrell 3-4× while leaning on its own disconnect-prone session check-in as the guard — during which a >30-min stale window occurred. VERIFIED (not asserted, DR-0076): searched the GitHub MCP toolset — there is no set-variable/secret tool, so the agent genuinely cannot arm it; that "can't" holds. But the ways-review found the bigger error: the healer is bounded, idempotent, single-flight, spawns no Claude/compute — NOT the runaway class P11 was written for — and is operationally identical to the db-migrate + auto-merge deploy dispatches that already ship ACTIVE ungated. The agent over-applied the letter of the three-brakes rule to the wrong class and manufactured the friction (SURFACE-PREMISE: "must be armed by hand" was self-imposed). ConnectBot's real relevance: it is Darrell's reliable command-runner (always-on, unlike the cloud session) — so an arm step is runnable from his phone, but the better fix is to not need the step. FRICTION -> action: Darrell chose option A — ungate it. Shipped: deploy-freshness.yml now runs by default (`if: vars.DEPLOY_FRESHNESS_ENABLED != 'false'`), keeps all three brakes (budget, concurrency lock, an opt-out kill-switch), so poetech.us self-heals with no arming and no session in the path; the manual check-in retires. Recorded as DR-0110 (revises DR-0109 §3). Standing lesson: challenge a self-imposed hoop before handing the principal a recurring manual step.
- **Source:** .github/workflows/deploy-freshness.yml

### REV-0011 · Post-feature alignment — do the pages still reflect reality after the Successor feature?
- **Date:** 2026-07-06
- **Surface:** Relationships (app/src/components/Relationships.jsx, the live Matrix) + sibling role/finance-visibility copy (FamilyRoster.jsx, datasystems-course.js, adopter-onboarding.js) after DR-0111 (the `successor` role + `finance.manage` capability + 0082 books RLS)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged

The trigger was Darrell holding the live Relationships → Guardian↔Child matrix (build DA630F3) asking "is this still accurate or has the feature updated it?" ALIGNED (verified against the running build + the model): (1) the Matrix panel is DERIVED-LIVE from lib/relationships.js — it renders `rel.roles.map` over `buildMatrix()`, so the **Family circle** tab now AUTO-shows a 4th `successor` column with `See family finances: Allowed` + `Work the books: Not allowed` (the read-only rung), and the Family-circle blurb naming the successor renders automatically. No hand-edit was needed — the "no painted permissions" design (DR-0076) is exactly what kept the surface from drifting. (2) The screenshot Darrell held (Guardian↔Child sub-tab) is UNCHANGED and accurate — `finance.manage` is a family capability, correctly absent from the guardian↔child matrix. (3) STRONGEST WIN — FamilyRoster's copy "a child... walled out of the family financials by row-level security... a minor can never see the books or the bank feed" was, before this feature, an ASPIRATIONAL claim the RLS did not actually enforce (the books gated on membership, not role — the child could read/write at the DB layer); 0082 made it TRUE, so the feature turned a false-ish claim honest rather than stale. INCOMPLETE (not wrong), tracked (DR-0075): the datasystems-course lessons (953/990) TEACH the relationship/role model ("a guardian and a child, family members, a landlord and a tenant") and do not yet mention the new `successor` read-only rung — pedagogically worth adding, not inaccurate; adopter-onboarding's `whatYouGet` lists the model generally ("family" covers the successor). **re-review 2026-07-13** — add the successor rung to the datasystems-course role lesson. STANDING PRACTICE now in force: a post-feature alignment review appends here after each feature/upgrade (the surface-reflects-reality half of DR-0104's live-production review + DR-0102's cadence). Durable lesson: surfaces that RENDER FROM THE MODEL (not hardcoded role lists) self-align — prefer them, and this review is where the non-dynamic copy gets swept.

### REV-0012 · Ways-review — the sideways-sub-tabs sweep: an untracked directive stalls; the parallel-crew lane worked
- **Date:** 2026-07-07
- **Surface:** How the DR-0116 sweep ran (8 parallel conversion crews over ~30 surfaces, per-batch commits, the auto-merge lane) + why the 2026-07-04 directive stalled at 8/39 surfaces
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Darrell had to ask a THIRD time for sliding tabs on all surfaces ("this is the second time I'm asking") — the ways failure behind it: the 2026-07-04/05 directive was captured only in the SectionTabs.jsx header comment, with no DR, no coverage checklist, no re-review date, so the remaining ~30 surfaces were never a tracked backlog and the rollout silently stopped (the DR-0075 "nothing stagnates silently" failure, and a cousin of P28 parking — work stopped without the governor saying stop). FIX SHIPPED: DR-0116 records the directive with a binding coverage checklist (session note 2026-07-07-sideways-sub-tabs-sweep.md lists every surface converted/harmonized/kept-with-why/fit-exempt) + "new surfaces adopt at birth." WHAT WORKED (keep): (1) Darrell-ordered ways-and-documentation review BEFORE changes (4 parallel research passes, 17 min) surfaced the guard landscape (tab-overflow invariant 3, Choir's scraped TABS, monolith budget, help-freshness fingerprints) so ZERO gates went red across the whole sweep; (2) 8 parallel crews on disjoint file sets with one shared conversion contract — request→all-code-pushed in 34 min wall clock with 407 files / 4,885 tests green and 16 new proof tests; (3) per-batch commits let the auto-merge lane land verified slices continuously (PRs #661/#662/#664) instead of one big-bang merge. FRICTIONS → actions: (1) tree-global guard snapshots (legibility-health.json) flap when parallel crews run guards mid-edit — the assembler re-runs the guard suite LAST on the assembled tree (done; keep as the standing pattern); (2) six surfaces could not fold into SectionTabs because their tab state is program-driven — a controlled-mode option would close them; recorded with re-review 2026-07-21 rather than forced; (3) the stop-hook's commit pressure during parallel crews was answered with completed-slice commits only — never committing sibling crews' mid-edit files; keep that discipline.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0116-sideways-sub-tabs-on-every-surface-no-buried-content.md

METHOD (DR-0076): observed pass on the RUNNING build — local production build served + Playwright chromium at 390x844 with a DOM audit (tap-target heights, duplicate labels, input attributes) + screenshots — plus a static sweep of every input surface. ### REV-0014 · Ways-review — the no-static-data pass: failures worked, records derived, the report made perpetual
- **Date:** 2026-07-07
- **Surface:** How the DR-0121/DR-0122 pass ran (the failure-fix sweep, the BuildBoard consolidation, Ari's derived record, the Perpetual Report) + the opportunities and constraints found
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Ways-review per DR-0108/DR-0120 §5, run with the work (Darrell 2026-07-07: "when we add features we need to update our Ways and documentation and find the opportunities and constraints"). WORKED THIS PASS: (1) all 4 open machine-audit findings FIXED same session (Church Home coming-soon dead-ends → honest real-action copy; CRM/Library/Scripture long lists → real windowing) — re-audit run, 0 open, 4 auto-resolved (the artifact is the proof, not a claim); (2) DR-0121 item 5 CLOSED — BuildBoard's hand-typed ROADMAP retired, the ship story now derives from the decision ledger + live boards + governance queue (lib/build-story.js, fixture-tested); (3) DR-0121 item 3's surface half CLOSED — Ari's notes/duties/workload derive live in Discussions (lib/ari-notes.js), the structural fix for "Ari has stopped updating as we add features"; (4) the Perpetual Report shipped (DR-0122) — the Books-ledger pattern generalized, CSV-portable, with the failures & fixes coverage strip as the standing report Darrell asked for. THE LOAD-BEARING FIND (a ways defect caught by doing, not by review): 28 DR files — including every record from DR-0116 on — used the list-style header the build parser could not read, so the newest decisions carried NO date/title into the app; every "derived" surface reading the ledger would have silently under-reported the newest work while looking perfectly live. That is P30's cousin: DERIVED-BUT-UNPARSED is a new staleness face — a derivation is only as honest as its parser's coverage of the real record. Fix shipped (both header shapes parse); standing check: when a repo record format drifts, the parser must follow in the same PR. CONSTRAINTS (verified, carried): the build agent still cannot write family-instance rows (RLS by design) — Ari's real synced reflections and the seed-concern drain stay with the Tier-C Local-LLM tending lane; signed-out devices show honest emptiness for synced streams; lessons incidents record "distilled," not "fixed" — the coverage lens reports what the record says, never an invented fix state. OPPORTUNITIES (ranked): (1) new record streams (delay ledger, ops commands, client engagements) join the Perpetual Report as projections — one PR each; (2) the CSV export can grow a direct hand-off into other business systems via the AI-Foundation lane; (3) the finish-ripple pattern can stamp `done_at` onto board rows, giving the Build tab real per-item ship dates beyond the ledger; (4) arming the tending lane (Tier C, three brakes) closes items 2/4/6's data halves. re-review: 2026-07-14 with the DR-0121 checklist.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0122-perpetual-reports-portable-cross-system-history.md

### REV-0016 · Post-feature O&C — the sub-tabs sweep, the validation lane, and the download-latest button
- **Date:** 2026-07-07
- **Surface:** the three features shipped this session (DR-0116 sideways sub-tabs; DR-0119 validation lane; the DownloadLatest button + checkForLatest) — their opportunities and constraints, per the standing "when we add features, find the opportunities and constraints" directive (Darrell 2026-07-07; DR-0120/0121 encode the Way; this record runs it on this session's ships)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** UPTIME AFFIRMED FIRST (Darrell: "better to have an old site rather than the site being down or blocked"): DR-0107 stands and today's evidence honors it — every production deploy run completed success on the exact merged SHA (verified per merge), and the update button is stale-beats-down BY CONSTRUCTION: it can only apply a build the browser has already fully downloaded; a failed check leaves the running (old) build untouched; a proven-not-to-stick device gets the honest close-and-reopen hint, never a blocked screen. CONSTRAINTS (verified, named narrowly): (1) the cloud build agent cannot probe poetech.us or the Vercel previews (proxy CONNECT 403, tested both) — live verification of the update flow is the family's DR-0104 reviewer pass, and the sibling constraint "agent limits are not team limits" (DR-0108) applies; (2) Vercel free-tier preview deploys rate-limited at 100/day mid-session — PREVIEWS pause, production (Cloudflare) unaffected; on a rate-limited day the Tier B soak falls back to the local production build + the full gate suite, which is what shipped; (3) a browser without service workers gets the honest no-sw state — updates arrive with the site; (4) six surfaces could not fold their strips into SectionTabs because their tab state is program-driven (recorded, re-review 2026-07-21 — a controlled-mode primitive closes them). OPPORTUNITIES (each routed, none dangling): (a) the install offer on "you're on the latest" — ALREADY BUILT by the sibling session same-day (Darrell's "why can't it do both"); (b) per-section help keys for converted sub-tab surfaces — parked with the key-convention decision, re-review 2026-07-21; (c) validation lanes as the standing discovery form for every future client (DR-0119 standing-use clause — the factory's next client opens with lanes on day one); (d) the About front door's sub-tab conversion — held Tier C PR, re-review 2026-07-14; (e) auto-check-on-focus already exists (startUpdateChecks) — the button adds the human hand, no duplicate machinery needed. COMBINE-WHAT-MAKES-SENSE check: no new static data was introduced by these features (the lane derives from synced board_tasks; the button reads the injected build marker + live SW state; the sweep moved existing JSX); the one pre-existing static surface they touched (BuildBoard ROADMAP) was retired by DR-0122 the same day — the button now sits on a fully derived page.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0119-current-future-gap-decision-validation-lane.md
### REV-0017 · Ways-review — the downtime stretch: the site gets its own witness; unknown never reads as fresh
- **Date:** 2026-07-08
- **Surface:** The delivery lane's observability (site-health probe, incident ledger, OpsBoard Uptime strip, Ari's uptime duty) + how the ways themselves ran
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Ways-review per DR-0108, run with the work (Darrell 2026-07-08: "The PoeTech App is down again!!!! How many times today? Again when we add features we need to update our Ways and documentation..."). THE WAYS DEFECT: every safeguard added across 07-06/07-08 (deploy-heal, deploy-freshness, the race fix) watched the PIPELINE — none ever made an HTTP request to the site the family opens, so "down" had no witness and "how many times today" had no answer (P31, DR-0125). WORKED THIS PASS: (1) the outside-in probe shipped and RAN — first real observation 03:06Z: root 302 → shell 200 → the shell's own bundle 200 → pages.dev 200, server-side UP; (2) the day reconstructed from real timestamps: two stale windows (~37 min, ~14 min) + ten deploys' client-side skew windows — the honest answer to "how many times," and the reason his device could break while the ledger read green; (3) the downtime ledger is now a queryable record (the rolling `incident` issue) surfaced LIVE on OpsBoard beside the lane state, through the SAME shared ETag/rate-budget path as github-ops (one budget, not two); (4) Ari's standing duties gained the uptime watch (DR-0125) — resolved against the live ledger like every other duty. CAUGHT-BY-ITS-OWN-DISCIPLINE: the probe's first run read served-build "unknown" and summarized "Fresh" — the painted-green class DR-0076 exists for; fixed in the same session (double-quote SW_VERSION stamp; unknown falls back to the deploy ledger and NEVER reads fresh). Proven-to-catch: the probe validated itself by failing honestly on its first fire. CONSTRAINTS (verified, carried): the cloud sandbox has NO route to poetech.us (egress 403 on direct + every relay) — the GitHub runner is the team's eye (WAYS-REVIEW: the agent's limits never bound the team); GitHub schedule crons throttle under load, so the ~10-min cadence is best-effort and the record states its own gaps; the unauthenticated 60/hr API budget holds the in-app read to mount + manual refresh. OPPORTUNITIES (ranked, in DR-0125): (1) announce-not-just-record (push alert on incident) re-review 2026-07-15; (2) deploy-churn batching to shrink client skew windows re-review 2026-07-15; (3) the incident ledger as a live Perpetual Report stream re-review 2026-07-22. SECOND FIND, same night, from Darrell's own screenshot: the header's alive-now DATE had frozen at mount (D20b regressed — a once-computed useMemo beside a ticking time), so a current app LOOKED stale; fixed by deriving date+time from one ticking clock, source-pinned in header-date-alive.test.js. The lesson joined LESSONS 2026-07-08: an alive-now label must name the ticking source it derives from, or it is a painted number with a delay.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0125-outside-in-site-health-probe-and-uptime-record.md

### REV-0018 · Ways-review — the Word-first Learn stretch: 104 derived sessions, the level switch that wasn't branching, and the house's welcome
- **Date:** 2026-07-08
- **Surface:** How the DR-0126/DR-0127 stretch ran (the derived processing courses, the Scripture level fix, the Word-first lead, the Engagement welcome) + opportunities and constraints
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Run with the work (DR-0108/DR-0120 s.5). SHIPPED: (1) six Learn courses DERIVED from the Godhead-Study catalog (104 sessions; a catalog pattern joins its course next build — pinned by a drift test); (2) every session multi-level by depth selection and Word-first by construction (the big idea OPENS with the references + Yahweh's own conditional — pinned); (3) the Learn header renders a derived Word-first lead for EVERY course (wordFirstLead: declared lead else first anchor; none = honest nothing + a finding); (4) the Engagement thread opens with the house's standing welcome + filler prompts (clearly the house speaking, never a painted user message). THE LOAD-BEARING FIND (P30's next face — SWITCH-WITHOUT-BRANCHES): the Scripture study's child/new-believer/scholar switch rendered for all 20 themes while 12 had NO authored level variants — every level silently read the standard adult text. A control that renders without content to branch to is stale in disguise: the check must verify the CONTENT branches, not that the switch renders. Fixed structurally (resolveLevel derives child <- essential, scholar <- deep from the theme's own authored tiers, authored variants senior, adaptedFrom carried; pinned across all 20 themes). MEASURED before designing (DR-0076): the coverage gap was counted from the real THEMES corpus (12/20 without variants), not assumed from the screenshot. CONSTRAINTS (held): level texts select authored words — child-VOICED retellings are SME work, never machine-invented doctrine; verse text renders by reference from the verified KJV corpus. OPPORTUNITIES (ranked): (1) witness rooms + study seeds as further derived courses on the same engine (re-review 2026-07-22); (2) derived quizzes per pattern (re-review 2026-07-29); (3) SME-authored level variants land over the derived fallback with zero code change (re-review 2026-07-22). Ari's record, the Build tab, and the Perpetual Report carry DR-0126/0126 by construction.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0126-learn-carries-the-eternal-algorithms-as-processing-courses.md

### REV-0019 · Ways-review — never trust a piped exit code: two masked failures in one night
- **Date:** 2026-07-08
- **Surface:** The verification lane itself — how the agent runs gates in the sandbox (vitest, eslint) before pushing
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** Ways-review per DR-0108, recorded as promised the same night it bit. THE DEFECT (twice in one session): `npx vitest run | tail` and `npm run lint | tail -1` both returned TAIL's exit code (0), not the gate's — a 2-test failure and the `'userTier' is not defined` lint failure both read as green locally and were caught only by CI on the pushed PR. That is the exact painted-green class DR-0076 exists for, applied to the agent's own process: a piped gate is a gate whose verdict has been replaced by the pipe's. THE WAY (binding on the agent's runs from here): (1) run gates with their exit codes VISIBLE — no bare `| tail` / `| head` on a command whose pass/fail matters; when output must be trimmed, capture the exit code first (`cmd > log; echo exit=$?; tail log`) or use the tool's own reporter/summary flags; (2) a local "green" that was piped is NOT evidence — re-run clean before claiming it (DR-0076 rule 1: no claim without evidence, and a masked exit code is no evidence). Both failures were fixed and re-verified with visible exit codes before merge (#695 landed green); the cost was one extra CI round-trip each — cheap this time, but the same mask on a deploy-lane script would be a P25-class miss. Pairs with LESSONS P31 (the witness must observe the real thing) — here the real thing is the gate's own verdict.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0076-verification-doctrine-trust-nothing-unverified.md

### REV-0020 · Ways-review — the DR-0107 watch caught its own lane fix being wrong (token-suppressed `closed`; the 2-minute poll)
- **Date:** 2026-07-08
- **Surface:** The auto-merge → deploy hand-off (.github/workflows/auto-merge.yml) — how the "merge deploys itself" mechanism actually fires
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** addressed
- **Findings:** DR-0107's watch-the-next-merge duty, held on PRs #695 and #697, corrected the DR-0128 lane change the same night it shipped. THE FINDS: (1) the `pull_request: closed` trigger added so "the merge itself runs the deploy" NEVER fires for auto-merges — the native auto-merge close is a GITHUB_TOKEN action, and GitHub suppresses workflow runs for EVERY event a GITHUB_TOKEN action emits (`push` was already known — P25; `closed` is the same rule wearing a new face); (2) #695 deployed hands-off anyway, but via the armed-PR wait loop in a `check_suite` sweep — the mechanism was right by luck, the attribution wrong; (3) #697 exposed the poll's real gap: the arming sweep fires at PR-OPEN when CI has only just started, so the 2-minute window expired a beat before the ~3-minute CI went green — no later event fired, and the deploy was dispatched by the watching agent (run 28917841247, success on 901f42b; ~5 minutes stale, healed before any family device met it). FIX SHIPPED: the wait loop polls 6 minutes (a full CI run + queue margin from the earliest arming moment; runs only while armed PRs exist, superseding sweeps cancel it); the `closed` comment now states the truth (human merges/closes only); DR-0128 carries a dated correction section. THE WAY: any future lane trigger is checked against the GITHUB_TOKEN suppression rule BEFORE it is trusted, and a lane change's DR-0107 watch is not one merge — it holds until the mechanism (not just the outcome) is confirmed. The corrected chain: armed-PR wait loop (primary) → `closed` sweep (human merges) → deploy-freshness cron (outer net).
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0128-relationships-rides-the-business-tier-and-merges-deploy-themselves.md

### REV-0026 · Ways-review — why the app doesn't feel self-healing; what's not working in the skills, from the orchestration record
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The whole healing/health inventory (workflows + in-app instruments), the orchestration record (all prior REVs, LESSONS P15-P31, the delay + unnecessary-ask ledgers), and the 335-video corpus gap as the type specimen (DR-0135)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE ANSWER TO "WHY NOT SELF-HEALING," MEASURED: the shell plane heals itself (chunk-reload, boot-fallback, site probe, deploy-freshness — all ACTIVE) but the DATA plane is detect-and-tell-a-human everywhere (loop-health, db-health, harvest-stall, photo-source-health all detect with NO actuator; transcript-backfill's schedule ships commented out; the intended tending actuator is deliberately kill-switched per the three-brakes law). The system watches honestly, then waits. THE TYPE SPECIMEN: the choir corpus — 0013 seeded 125 of 335 channel videos; one filter() line dropped every undated title; the debt was recorded only inside the migration's own comment; NO instrument compared channel-vs-app; the family photographed what the instruments never measured. Also found and fixed: the title parser truncated real message titles at apostrophes ('I', 'YOU CAN' shipped in 0013 rows) — regression-pinned. THE SKILLS FINDINGS (from the full record): output failures got machine gates and stopped recurring; the recurring classes are process-shaped — (A) built-but-never-surfaced/silent partial completion (Learn catalog "1000th time"; sideways tabs 8-of-39; this corpus gap), (B) claimed-not-verified-in-the-failure-mode (piped exit codes twice in one night; "wrote an error row" counted as progress), (D/F) parking + re-asking + directives repeated until made structural, (G) the agent scoping to its own reach (ConnectBot). WHY DIRECTIVES REPEAT, located: capture (written where it can't be tracked), survival (context compaction), application (principles with no end-of-turn checkpoint) — and the one directive that STOPPED repeating is the one made derived-by-construction (feature lands WITH its record). THE PROGRAM (DR-0135): probe → derived readout → actuator → announce as the standard for every failure class; corpus wholeness shipped as the first instrument (manifest + strip + dispatch-only reconcile workflow); the remaining gaps routed with dates (read-outcome envelope 07-24; announce path + data-plane probe + detector actuators 07-31; cross-device aggregation 08-14). CONSTRAINTS: scheduled actuators stay Tier C under the three brakes; the un-gate-able classes (parking, re-asks, self-scoping) have no CI gate by nature — their ledgers are the control and the counts are KPIs.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0135-self-healing-program-probe-readout-actuator-announce.md

(3) Issue 4 teaches discernment for PROPHETIC/fear-framed tech content — a different challenge than Issue 1: Musk's alarm-language is DOCUMENTED and said plainly ("summoning the demon," MIT Oct 2014, WaPo/CBS; "potentially more dangerous than nukes," his Aug 2014 post; the WSJ Apr 2025 "legion... before the apocalypse" texts, Forbes/Axios — all re-verified by live web search 2026-07-10 with as-of dates); the alleged DELETED Lex-Fridman-2027 segment is labeled at its true weight — ZERO (unfalsifiable by construction; negative search result recorded as a source); the Word educates past the fear (no date-setting, Matthew 24:36; try the spirits, 1 John 4:1; the Babel pattern, Genesis 11:4; the true Guide, John 16:13; a sound mind, 2 Timothy 1:7; look up, Luke 21:28) while KEEPING the creator's true warning (the AI counterfeit-shepherd concern stands — DR-0100 runs both ways); the DR-0130 two-courts accountability block names the creator's witness-accountability (Matthew 12:36), Musk's unruled-on record entering the eternal court (Ecclesiastes 12:14), the platforms' false balance (Proverbs 11:1), and OUR accountability for what we repeat and what we mute. Audit gate: ALL FOUR issues pass (labels, dated sources, steelmanned perspectives, grace note, stated accountability). ### REV-0029 · Post-feature O&C + ways — the whole watchable history surfaces; the door heals itself before it asks
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The Choir history (buildPastServices + the derived Show-more paging), the boot-fallback self-heal ladder every device meets, Ari's duties + derived notes (DR-0137)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DIRECTIVE RAN WHOLE (Darrell 2026-07-10, two screenshots): "The church app should never go down. Also the Choir tab still only has 7 videos in the history dropdown" + the standing frame (Ways/docs/O&C/Ari updated with the feature, in-app, no static data). THE TRACE ANSWERED HONESTLY: (1) the "down" screenshot is boot-fallback's "Almost there — one more tap" screen — the site was serving; a deploy-window stale shell (the DR-0128 class) put a TAP on the family that a machine can run itself; (2) the 7-video history is the planned-or-setlist filter in buildPastServices — the test even pinned the exclusion ("avoids 130 empty cards") — hiding every recorded-but-songless service in the corpus DR-0135 just made measurable: built-but-never-surfaced, at the display layer, exactly the recurring class REV-0026 named. SHIPPED: (1) a recorded service IS history — keep planned OR setlist OR watchable; the toggle count derives from the whole corpus; the old exclusion pin REPLACED with a watchable-inclusion pin + a pure-noise exclusion pin; (2) derived paging — "Show more (N more)" states the remainder, every service reachable, no fixed cap, no hundreds-of-cards mount on a phone; (3) the self-heal ladder — auto-reload, then auto cache-clear+reload, then (and only then) the manual screen; attempts stamped in sessionStorage in a 90s window (the chunk-heal pattern); storage-less environments go STRAIGHT to manual (cannot count = never loop — the brake); the healing screen asks for nothing (role=status, no buttons) and the manual copy now tells the truth ("We refreshed automatically and your device is still holding the old version"); (4) ARI + REPORTS WITH THE FEATURE — the watchable-history standing duty resolves against DR-0137 live, the derived notes pick the DR up this build, this REV files the pass. WAYS-REVIEW QUESTIONS: capability not used — the fallback screen had two machine-runnable recoveries waiting on a human hand (Drive-Don't-Delegate applies to the app's own surfaces, not just the agent); unverified can't — none asserted; repeated friction absorbed — "the app is down" sightings that are really stale-shell taps now heal silently, and the family's screenshot loop on this class should end. CONSTRAINTS: two auto attempts per window maximum (a genuinely broken deploy lands on the manual screen, not a spinning phone); sandbox has no route to poetech.us — pinned suites prove locally, the family's DR-0104 pass confirms live. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0137 with dates): heal events into the error journal → OpsBoard shell-plane KPI 2026-07-24; sermon title/speaker on video-only history cards 2026-07-24.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0137-the-watchable-history-is-whole-and-the-door-heals-itself.md

### REV-0030 · Post-feature O&C — the voices made true: the "never worked" diagnosed, the women heard, the vendor recorded
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The whole speech stack (tts.js, use-read-aloud, the dramatized cast player, scripture-voices/cast, voice-service, VoiceStudio), the sovereignty ledger, Ari's voices duty (DR-0138)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** "MY AI VOICE NEVER WORKED," ANSWERED WITH EVIDENCE: every backend beyond browser speech was inert — the vendor bridge had no token/flag, the sovereign XTTS studio on the 4070 was written but never armed (device register: ":8770 not probed") — so the "AI voice" was always a labeled browser stand-in with no real backend on any device; beneath it, the cold-start tap (empty getVoices resolves NO voice → raw OS default, wrong gender) and the dramatized read's second, un-hardened speech path (bulk-queue iOS truncates; unreferenced utterances Chrome GCs; bare pre-cancel swallows the first tap). SHIPPED: (1) the floor hardened — the cast player now speaks sequentially through the same defenses as the main engine (segmentText, GC-retain, busy-only cancel, resume kick, onerror-advance; addEventListener not property-clobber for voiceschanged), and waitForVoices closes the cold-start gap with resolution against what actually arrived; (2) THE WOMEN HEARD — Mary (Luke 1:38, 46-47) and the faithful women (Elizabeth Luke 1:42; Ruth 1:16; Martha John 11:27) join the red-letter palette and are CAST to distinct female device voices beside the men cast male; every quote fetched verbatim from the shipped KJV, the existing verbatim gate proves it in CI; unverified speech stays the narrator's — never guessed; (3) SOVEREIGN FIRST, VENDOR RECORDED (the sharpened directive, both halves) — the studio on our own 4070 always outranks the bridge; lib/sovereignty-gaps.js is the standing ledger (voice-clone: build path = arm the studio, NO purchase needed; big-LLM: the planned rig, purchase gated on research; video-gen: recorded ahead of use) where a vendor need without its full record (when needed, local-today, build, purchase, re-review) FAILS THE BUILD (proven-to-catch), and vendor playback announces itself in the notice; (4) the stand-in now SAYS what turns the real voice on, ending the silent "never worked" experience. CONSTRAINTS: the sandbox cannot hear audio — the family's DR-0104 pass on real phones is the ear; Android pause stays best-effort; device voice sets vary and the engine never promises a timbre it cannot verify. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0138): arm the studio 2026-07-24; grow the verified speaker book 2026-08-07; generalize the ledger to the DR-0132 provider slots 2026-08-07.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0138-voices-true-on-every-device-sovereign-first-vendor-recorded.md
### REV-0031 · Comprehensive UI/UX + quality + self-healing review — the crash diagnosed in a real browser; healing made visible; "boots" becomes the deploy bar
<!-- renumbered from REV-0030 (collision with the voices O&C entry above) -->

- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The whole app (three parallel review sweeps: UI/UX 25 findings, self-healing inventory 15 instruments, static-data 15 findings), the self-heal wiring every device runs, the deploy lane's verify-boot job, the quality board (DR-0139)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE LIVE QUESTION ANSWERED WITH A REPRODUCTION, NOT A THEORY: while the review ran, Darrell photographed the app down ("even in incognito") with every probe/deploy/CI signal green. The exact main SHA was built locally and driven in real Chromium: THE DEPLOYED CODE MOUNTS — and the same run reproduced the on-device truth-destroyer: chunk-heal's unconditional preventDefault makes Vite SWALLOW a failed import's rejection, the import resolves undefined, and main.jsx's "Cannot destructure property 'default' of 'undefined'" replaces the real cause. The instruments were green because nothing executes the JS — CI is jsdom, the probe is curl. SHIPPED (DR-0139): (1) prevent-default only when healing (gave-up lets the truth reject through to the boundary + journal); (2) can't-count → don't-loop unified (no-storage chunk-heal gives up like the boot ladder); (3) every self-heal journaled (kind 'heal': chunk-heal, each boot rung, landed SW updates; the exhausted ladder journals as a real error) and COUNTED — "Self-heals (this device)" on Quality & Throughput, heals excluded from the error roll-up, which answers "why doesn't it SEEM self-healing": the healing was real and invisible; (4) verify-boot — scripts/boot-check.mjs in headless Chrome against production after every deploy, proven-to-catch (FAILED on a boot-broken build: "recovery screen shown", exit 1; PASSED on the mounting build), filing the rolling incident ledger on failure; (5) the photographed '&QUOT;' choir title decodes (parser + mappers, stored rows heal at render); (6) the two already-drifted literals derive (the "~46" library label — registry actually 49; "All 11 rentals" → rentals.length). THE REVIEW'S SYSTEMIC CLASSES, ROUTED: UI/UX — forked design tokens (36px targets, drifted hexes, an off-system games surface), native alert()/confirm() as the feedback layer leaking raw e.message to church members, ~600 frozen-px text sizes defeating A+++ large print — each a class fix (shared tokens module, in-app alert/confirm primitives, a px-text lint rule), re-review 2026-07-17; static-data — ChurchVideoWall hand-typed timeline/status, "77th National Assembly", duplicated clinician counts, tour counts, SURFACE_CATALOG, re-review 2026-07-17; self-healing data plane — every needed actuator already BUILT but unconnected (harvest-stall→transcript wire, corpus-reconcile FIRST dispatch with manifest still generatedAt:null, failed-migration→db-migrate re-dispatch, pinned-LLM→unload job, the announce path to family phones), dates held per DR-0135 (07-24/07-31). CONSTRAINTS: verify-boot is non-required (visibility, not a merge brake — the gates remain the brake, DR-0103); the sandbox has no route to production, so the diagnosis method (build the SHA, drive it in Chromium) is now ALSO the production instrument. WAYS: the second ask of an already-answered question was treated as the signal it is — the structural answer had shipped, the VISIBLE answer had not; visibility is part of self-healing, not decoration on it.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0139-boots-in-a-real-browser-is-the-bar-and-healing-is-visible.md

### REV-0032 · Post-feature O&C + ways — the builder's road adopted: one playbook teaching and running, heuristics named, nothing static
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** Kingdom Economics session 8 (econ8-test-build-multiply), the client-acquisition engine (GROWTH_FRAMEWORKS in the stage prompts) + the Client Growth adopted-plays strip, Ari's growth-plays duty (DR-0140), the REV ledger itself (the REV-0030 collision cleaned)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DIRECTIVE RAN WHOLE (Darrell 2026-07-10): a Daniel Priestley long-form interview brought as build input — "Lesson or another Way to create wealth... research and adoption for what makes sense for our business operation... Ways and documentation... opportunities and constraints... Ari's responsibility and reports... all inside the PoeTech App. No static data." PROCESSED PER DR-0100's THREE TIERS, not swallowed and not staged as a debate: demand-testing adopted whole (it IS Proverbs 24:27 / Luke 14:28 and DR-0076 applied to commerce — real names before real capital, and the test itself must be honest, no fake scarcity); the 7-11-4 rule adopted AS A NAMED HEURISTIC on every surface that carries it (the biblical constant beneath is faithful sowing, Galatians 6:9); personal branding adopted WITH THE WORD'S CORRECTION (identity in Christ; a good NAME stewarded — Proverbs 22:1, another's mouth praises — Proverbs 27:2); Name-Same-Fame-Aim-Game adopted (a certain sound, 1 Corinthians 14:8); the leverage ladder adopted with PoeTech as the family's own worked example (skills → teaching → media → the app); environment adopted (Proverbs 13:20; module 4 already named the Body as the first room). SHIPPED AS ONE PLAYBOOK FROM ONE SOURCE (DR-0121): the lesson (session 8, every KJV fragment verbatim-gated against the shipped KJV in CI — a new pinned gate proves the quotes), the engine (GROWTH_FRAMEWORKS woven into the stage prompts AFTER the guardrails — order pinned in tests), and the surface (the adopted-plays strip rendering the same data, heuristic labels and honesty bends visible). ARI + REPORTS WITH THE FEATURE: the growth-plays standing duty resolves against DR-0140 live; the derived notes pick the DR up this build; this REV files the pass. CLEANING (keep cleaning until we like it): the REV-0030 numbering collision found in the registry — the comprehensive review renumbered to REV-0031 with its DR-0139 and INDEX references updated; the voices O&C keeps REV-0030 (its session note already points there). WAYS-REVIEW QUESTIONS: capability not used — the whole KJV lives in-repo, so verse verification needed no web at all; unverified can't — outbound web asserted blocked only after three verified 403s, and the constraint carried with a re-review rather than fabricated URLs; repeated friction absorbed — "lesson + operation drift apart" is structurally closed by the one-source pattern. CONSTRAINTS (held): Priestley book/URL attribution is training-data-flagged, unverifiable from this sandbox (re-review 2026-07-24); paid-ads demand testing NOT adopted — real money on real strangers is a Governor decision when a real offer is ready; 7-11-4 stays a heuristic or gets verified before any surface states it as fact. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0140 with dates): a real waiting-list surface on the signup-metrics seam 2026-07-24; the book flywheel's lead magnets doubling as demand evidence 2026-08-07; a 7-11-4-shaped honest content cadence (three-brakes if timer-driven) 2026-08-07.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0140-the-builders-road-adopted-growth-frameworks-tested-against-the-word.md

### REV-0034 · Post-feature O&C — the Council Chamber door opens the Chamber, pinned structurally
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The church home's section-aware launch targets (six chamber doors), the Learn launch labels, the Library link resolver, the structural launch-intent gate (DR-0142)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE TRACE (Darrell 2026-07-10, screenshot of the Worship video where the Chamber should be): "Open the Council Chamber" targeted plain church home; the home's tabs default to Worship (latest-message autoplay); the Chamber — real and live as the Speak section's one-voice input (DR-0131) — was unreachable by ANY launch target because targets couldn't name a section; the label function blessed every home target as the Chamber. SHIPPED: (1) targets carry churchSection, honored by BOTH resolvers (the Learn launcher and the Library navigator); the Live-Worship bar explicitly resets to the stream default because that shortcut IS the stream; (2) all six chamber-intent doors (AI class wk1 first-prompt, AI-Legal sovereignty ×2, broadcast captions drill, sovereign-AI tutor, book-engine testimony — a testimony is SPOKEN) open the speak section; the OBS switching lesson keeps Worship on purpose; (3) the label never claims a door it doesn't open — chamber label ONLY with the speak target, plain home reads "church home"; (4) THE CLASS IS MACHINE-DEAD, not instance-fixed: council-chamber-launch.test.js sweeps EVERY course module and fails the build if a lesson that names the Chamber/"our own A.I." doesn't launch into it — with a non-vacuous floor so an import drift can't silently empty the sweep. CONSTRAINTS: a section launch remounts the home surface (transient form state resets; launches always arrive from another tab); the URL doesn't carry the section yet. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0142, 2026-07-24): section-in-URL (?sec=speak) so a texted link opens the Chamber; the Chamber's header naming itself "Council Chamber" beside "Yahweh Hears You" so the arriving learner knows they've landed. WAYS: the label-vs-door mismatch is REALITY-TRACE at the navigation layer (the button promised a surface it never showed), and the fix is a standing gate, not a one-time correction. Numbering note: landed as DR-0142/REV-0034 after two sibling sessions took DR-0140/0141 and REV-0031..33 mid-flight — the ledger's read-Next-ID-from-live-main rule (DR-0052) applied at merge.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0142-a-door-that-names-the-chamber-opens-the-chamber.md

### REV-0035 · Research Day pass #1 — the sourcing bench built, the intake made structural, the house record overruling the trend
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** Research Day (DR-0143): the sourcing bench + intake + findings registry (lib/research-intake.js), the bench/cadence strip in Ari's record (Discussions), Ari's sourcing duty, the staged weekly Routine
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DIRECTIVE RAN WHOLE (Darrell 2026-07-10, across one session): two video briefings brought as build input (NotebookLM 2.0; AI-orchestration — both hand-carried by his Gemini Pro, per the screenshots); "Ari should be able to source my gemini and chatgpt $20 accounts for certain skills claude doesnt have"; "Keep researching certain day/s to keep researching upgrades and features from GitHub"; the evidence sharpening ("test and see not agree without testing... adoption of what actually works not change just because"); and the n8n correction ("I don't like n8n... from experience... Ari should already know"). TWO CATCHES PROVED THE INTAKE THIS PASS: (1) PREMISE-VERIFY — the orchestration briefing asserted an "existing llm-worker.js", a Next.js architecture, WebLLM, and "Project SHALOM"; verified against the repo: the scaffold is dead (MVP-1-HARDENING-PLAN lists it for archive), the app is Vite + React, the rest is another project's framing — nothing was built on it; (2) HOUSE-FIRST / RECORDED-EXPERIENCE-IS-SENIOR — the ecosystem scan staged the n8n AI Agent node as "highest-leverage" on adoption signal, and the house's own record had already judged it (DR-0132 off the critical path; DR-0083; LESSONS P17–P19; the HTTP 530 night) — DECLINED, reopen only by a governance case on tests and outcomes, with a pinned test holding that nothing recommends n8n back onto the critical path. SHIPPED AS ONE SOURCE (DR-0121): SOURCING_BENCH (the whole team's instruments with honest limits — the consumer Gemini/ChatGPT tiers carry NO API, output enters hand-carried and premise-verified; the sovereign lane is the DR-0132 Supabase-bus + box, not n8n), INTAKE_STEPS (capture → premise-verify → house-first → tier → evidence-based verdict → one source → record → reflect → schedule), RESEARCH_FINDINGS (11 evidence-based verdicts, every non-adopted one dated), and researchCadence — the weekly Research Day MEASURED from this registry (a pass counts only when its REV carrying the marker is filed; no record reads OVERDUE, never fresh). ARI + REPORTS WITH THE FEATURE: the sourcing duty resolves against DR-0143 live; the bench, findings, and cadence render in Ari's record on Discussions; the derived notes pick the DR up this build; the re-review dates land in the sortable backlog automatically. CLEANING: this record and its DR renumbered from a REV-0033/DR-0141 collision with the parallel session's harvest pass (the input-manager DR-0141 + REV-0033/0034 landed on main first) — the INDEX's read-Next-ID-from-live-main rule held. WAYS-REVIEW QUESTIONS: capability not used — the team's bench itself was the gap (the agent had no recorded routing knowledge of Darrell's Gemini/NotebookLM/ChatGPT skills); unverified can't — "consumer accounts can't integrate" was verified precisely (no API on either $20 tier; the AI Studio free key is the honest Gemini unlock, quotas unverified, re-review 2026-07-24); repeated friction absorbed — hand-carried briefings now have a structural premise-verify gate instead of session alertness; scoped-to-own-limits — the bench names the principal (ConnectBot, the consumer tools) and the runners as instruments, per DR-0108. CONSTRAINTS (held): the weekly TIMER ships STAGED-INACTIVE with its three brakes (budget, single-instance skip, stop-on-failure) — timer-driven Claude compute never self-activates unattended (the 2026-06-06 rule); the Governor arms it with a word. re-review 2026-07-17 — the first due date of the measured cadence.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0143-the-sourcing-bench-multi-model-skills-and-the-measured-research-day.md
### REV-0036 · Post-feature O&C — the choir room holds Sundays; the words arrive as drafts; Ari's training loop opens
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The Choir history (Sunday-focused), the Draft-words action + auto-draft badge on set lists, Words on the Songbook card, lib/choir-words.js + lib/ari-words-training.js, Ari's words-teacher duty (DR-0144)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DIRECTIVE RAN WHOLE (Darrell 2026-07-10, two Choir screenshots + the sharpening): "choir only sings on Sunday", "we don't have the words for each one", "use one of the final versions from the choir to see the difference so Ari can be trained to tease out the words… so Ari is the expert at these." SHIPPED DERIVED: (1) the DR-0137 widened history focused — a video-only past service shows in the CHOIR room only on a real Sunday (weekday from the date, honoring the 2026-07-02 date-honesty rule; the FILTER changed, not the label truth); Wednesday Bible study and Monday-posted video-only rows stay in the sermon library; planned rows and rows with songs stay regardless (Christina's Monday conference set is hers); (2) Draft-words: one tap teases a starting sheet from the SAME live video_transcripts rows the sermon library and Call-to-Give read (DR-0134's recipe, third harvest from one source) — heard-quote anchor with title fallback, verse-width wrapping, the sheet labeled [Auto-draft… trim to the words the choir sings], inline honest states (no transcript yet → the loader fills these; not locatable → add by hand), NEVER a native alert (the REV-0031 class); the Songbook card shows Words wherever a rendition carries them; (3) ARI'S TRAINING LOOP: the label line IS the draft/final boundary (trimmed on confirmation — the text testifies, no flag column); correction pairs derive LIVE (confirmed final + same transcript re-teased → recall/precision), zero second-store; calibrationSummaryLine is measured or honestly empty — the corpus has zero confirmed sheets today, so NO calibration UI shipped painted (DR-0076): the loop that creates the first pairs shipped instead. PROVEN-TO-CATCH, TWICE: the new sweep runs green, AND the sibling session's consistency guard caught this very batch adding emoji-as-icon buttons — the review's class gate working on the reviewer within hours of shipping. CONSTRAINTS: transcripts hear ad-libs and the room — the draft is a quarry, said on the surface; transcript coverage is partial and counted (awaitingTranscript), never claimed. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0144): calibration readout surfaces at the first confirmed sheet, 2026-07-17; the correction-pair engine generalizes to UI/UX findings and quality controls (Tier-C tuning lane, three brakes), 2026-07-24.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0144-the-choir-room-holds-sundays-and-ari-learns-the-words.md

### REV-0037 · Post-feature O&C — the door opens itself and opens your way; the reconcile's first live run caught its own plumbing
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The heal ladder's manual rung (boot-fallback + bustReload), the sign-in door (PasswordAuth link-first), the corpus-reconcile lane, live-report triage across three device screenshots (DR-0145)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THREE LIVE REPORTS, EACH TRACED TO EVIDENCE BEFORE FIXING (DR-0076): (1) "the one tap doesn't work" — TRUE by construction: the manual screen's primary button repeated the plain reload the ladder had already auto-failed, and NO rung busted an HTTP/edge-cached index (the one holder clearAppCaches can't touch); fixed — primary tap = full clear + cache-busted fresh URL, pinned by a test that fails if it ever degrades to the bare reload; (2) "can't download without a password" — install was NEVER auth-gated and the password-free paths (Google, Royalty Link) + the full PIN/fingerprint/device-trust machinery were live but hidden behind a password-led form, against the BINDING commitment 2; fixed — the door defaults to name + email + the emailed link, password behind an explicit choice, the PIN/fingerprint promise stated on the door; (3) "why only ~138 videos" — the instrumented corpus gap; the reconcile was DISPATCHED (drive-don't-delegate), its first live run listed the full channel successfully on the runner and then failed on a trailing-whitespace secret (psql: database "postgres\n") — fixed with the migrate lane's own tr -d '[:space:]' pattern; re-dispatch follows the merge. THE RECURRING QUESTION ANSWERED STRUCTURALLY: "why doesn't it feel self-healing" asked twice in one day = the actuators exist but wait for a human dispatch by three-brakes design; the closing move is the governor arming the watched schedules (weekly reconcile, hourly transcript backfill), routed Tier C re-review 2026-07-17. WAYS NOTE: every fix in this batch started from a family screenshot — the announce path (DR-0135 gap, 07-31) is what retires the screenshot as the alerting mechanism. CONSTRAINTS: the sandbox cannot tap his phone — the fresh-copy button's live proof is his next tap (DR-0104); OTP link delivery depends on the Supabase redirect allow-list already configured.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0145-the-door-opens-itself-and-opens-your-way.md
### REV-0038 · Post-fix O&C — the ops lane reads true and the cleanup became a process the team can dispatch
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** The OpsBoard CI verdict + lane budget (lib/github-ops.js), the open-PR ledger, the new pr-janitor lane (DR-0146)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE BOARD'S "CI UNKNOWN" WAS THE BOARD MISREADING A BUSY LANE: the verdict came from workflow_runs[0], so a null-conclusion in-flight run erased the finished run's truth sitting on the same API page — fixed to judge from the most recent COMPLETED run with the in-flight run told beside it, pinned by tests (a knowable verdict reported "unknown" is an under-claim — DR-0100). THE LANE BUDGET WAS FEEDING CORPSES: 24 open PRs, default API order, 8 real-file lookups — the live work read "unknown" while zombies got described; evidence pass proved 23 of 24 branches empty vs main (git diff --quiet, branch by branch; #712 real with 22 files, kept). THE WAYS CORRECTION THAT SHAPED THE PACKAGE: hand-closing was interrupted by the governor mid-sweep ("comprehensive processes") — one PR had been closed by hand; the other 22 wait for pr-janitor.yml, the dispatch-only process that closes ONLY idle+provably-empty PRs (hold/draft/armed/real-changes always survive, reported in the run summary), 25-close budget + lock + no schedule per the three-brakes law. CONSTRAINTS HELD HONESTLY: the janitor ships inactive — arming its cadence rides the same standing Tier C decision as the other watched schedules (re-review 2026-07-17); the unauthenticated 60/hr API budget is kept at 8 lookups, now spent on the PRs that are actually alive.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0146-the-lane-tells-the-truth-and-cleans-up-after-itself.md
