### REV-0040 · Post-deploy O&C — the boot-check learns to follow the heal it is watching
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** scripts/boot-check.mjs (the deploy lane's real-browser verify), the 10:04Z deploy run for merged #724
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DEPLOY WAS GREEN AND THE INSTRUMENT RED: the Cloudflare deploy for 8315059 succeeded and the fresh build booted green on the origin (BOOT OK pages.dev), but every poetech.us URL failed with "Execution context was destroyed, most likely because of a navigation" — the edge was still serving the previous index for the first minutes after deploy, so the app's OWN stale-build heal ladder (reload, then cache-clear + cache-busted fresh URL — the very behavior #724 shipped) navigated while the judge held a reference to the old document. The instrument was failing the feature. FIX: the judge runs in a bounded follow-the-heal loop (NAV_FOLLOWS=4) — on a navigation teardown it settles on the new document and judges THAT; a mounted app after a self-heal is BOOT OK, the manual recovery screen stays an honest FAIL, and a ladder that never converges runs out of follows and fails loudly (DR-0076 — the check still catches real breaks; it just stops judging mid-flight). Third instrument lesson of the day in the same family: slow is not down (the 04:51Z run), the gate is a mount (#719), and now healed is not down. site-health was dispatched as the independent witness during diagnosis per DR-0125.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0139-boots-in-a-real-browser-is-the-bar-and-healing-is-visible.md
### REV-0041 · Post-process O&C — the janitor's first sweep measured, its guard caught lying, corrected same session
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** pr-janitor.yml first two dispatched runs; the open-PR ledger (DR-0148)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE FIRST RUN WAS MEASURED, NOT TRUSTED (DR-0076): both sweeps green in ~11-14s, 0 closed, open-PR count unchanged at 25 — the ~1.3s sweep step with zero per-branch fetches proved every stale PR hit an early continue. ROOT CAUSE: the "auto-merge armed = live" guard is false-premised here — the lane arms every auto-opened PR, so all 20+ zombies are armed-but-dead and the guard read each as alive. THE ANTI-THEATER PRINCIPLE FIRED EXACTLY AS WRITTEN: a gate that always passes is itself a lie; the process was corrected the same session (DR-0148 — armed status reported, never trusted; liveness = recency + runner-verified diff) and the next dispatch after merge does the real retirement. ALSO PROVEN THIS PASS: the corpus-reconcile lane ran green end-to-end after its secret-hygiene fix — full channel listed, idempotent backfill applied, choir_sermons_rows = 388 measured live, manifest PR pushed onto the lane.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0148-armed-is-not-alive-the-janitors-first-sweep-caught-its-own-guard.md

### REV-0044 · Post-feature O&C — the family's words save as they type (Christina's Google-Doc contract on the one input primitive)
- **Date:** 2026-07-10

- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE FAMILY'S FIRST WRITER ASKED (Christina 2026-07-10, "1. Book cover" typed and unsaved in her screenshot): "automatically save your notes like a google doc without hitting save… when you stall out with time or forget and come back, your information is still there." SHIPPED ON THE ONE PRIMITIVE (DR-0131 pays off — one fix covers every tab's box): (1) save-as-you-type — text + chosen destination chip + name persist device-locally per surface (350ms debounce); an emptied box clears its draft; (2) coming back restores the box exactly, the restored chip honored over the suggester, with the quiet notice "Your unsent words were kept — everything here saves as you type, no Save needed" (role=status, no popup); (3) a delivered Send clears the draft; (4) sovereign by construction — localStorage on her device; nothing egresses until she sends (the routing contract untouched). PINNED: the round-trip, per-surface isolation, corrupt/storage-less honesty, the stall-out remount restoring text + notice, and send-clears — all in draft-autosave.test.js. CONSTRAINTS (stated, not hidden): drafts are per-device (cross-device = a synced store = a separate Tier-B decision); private-mode browsers degrade to today's behavior, never a crash. OPPORTUNITIES (routed in DR-0151): the same contract on the other long-form editors (song words, sermon notes, feedback composer) 2026-07-17. WAYS: a family member's relayed sentence became a shipped contract + a DR + this record in one pass — the covenant that spoken requests get built (Spoken-Teachings doctrine, applied to the family's product voice).

### REV-0045 · Ways-review — the install cry: instruments before guesses, the device in hand over the UA on paper, and the sovereign door
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** How the install work ran (PRs #730/#732/#733 + DR-0152/DR-0153) — diagnosis order, screenshot-driven correction, the store lane opening
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** KEPT: measurement before theory — when "can't download" arrived, the first ship was an INSTRUMENT (install-health: Chrome's own installability verdict from a runner, DR-0125's eye), and its verdict (zero site-side errors) redirected the whole hunt from the manifest to the missing button; every fix landed with render tests and rode the lane to green. THE PRINCIPAL'S SCREENSHOTS OUTRAN THE INSTRUMENTS THREE TIMES: the blank page (P32 window), the "ON A COMPUTER" steps on his Fold, and the menu with no install entry — each screenshot corrected a premise no probe had tested. WAYS QUESTIONS: (1) capability not used — the runner could ask Chrome WHY all along (Page.getInstallabilityErrors existed; nobody had asked); (2) unverified "can't" — "the sandbox can't reach GitHub's API unauthenticated" was verified (proxy 403), and the MCP tools carried it; (3) repeated friction absorbed — DR numbering collided with sibling lanes THREE times on one PR (0147 taken mid-flight, then 0149/0150/0151 while the PR waited); the sharpened way: re-read the ledger's Next ID every time origin/main moves under an open PR, and rebase-time is a numbering checkpoint equal to write-time; (4) scoped-to-own-limits check — the Fold's desktop UA was invisible from any runner (a runner has no touch screen); the fix encodes the device-in-hand truth the UA hides. FRICTIONS → ACTIONS: the deploy-window class needs its serve-layer decision (incident #722, re-review 2026-07-12); install-health joining the site-health cadence (DR-0153, re-review 2026-07-24); boot-check filings during known windows bury real failures in expected red (fold into the same 07-12 decision).
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0153-the-install-door-is-first-class-and-measured.md

### REV-0046 · Correction — the "23 empty zombies" were never empty: a shallow clone's error masqueraded as evidence
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** the open-PR ledger; the pr-janitor evidence gate; the sandbox measurement that fed DR-0146/DR-0148
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE RECORD IS CORRECTED (DR-0076 — an over-claim is retracted the moment it is caught): the claim that "23 of 24 open PRs carry empty diffs vs main" — stated in DR-0146's trace and repeated in DR-0148 — was a MEASUREMENT ERROR, not a fact. The sandbox clone was SHALLOW; `git diff origin/main...branch` had no merge base and exited 128 (an ERROR), and the error was misread as a verdict. Re-measured on the unshallowed clone: ALL 24 open PRs carry real changes (4-135 files each). Two structural lessons now pinned in the workflow itself: (1) in a squash-merge repo a three-dot diff is essentially never empty — merged work lands as new squash commits — so the janitor's empty-diff gate is a CONSERVATIVE guard by construction and stale-with-real-changes PRs are a HUMAN triage list, never automation's; (2) an error is not a verdict — the gate now distinguishes "no merge base / could not judge" from both "empty" and "has changes". THE PROCESS ITSELF WAS RIGHT TWICE: runs 2 and 3 refused to close any of these PRs — the summary rows said "stale but CARRIES REAL CHANGES — left open for a human" and that was the truth while the session's own premise was wrong. The janitor's zero-close was not a failure to fix; it was the guard doing its job. DR-0146's decision stands (close only provably-empty); DR-0148's guard removal stands (armed is still not a liveness signal); only the zombie COUNT claim is retracted. The 20 un-held stale PRs (June-early-July parallel-session work) route to the Governor as a triage list with their file counts.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0148-armed-is-not-alive-the-janitors-first-sweep-caught-its-own-guard.md

### REV-0047 · Incident — every gate was green while every installed device was dead: the worker's own navigation had no witness
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** app/public/sw.js (the #750 cache-first shell → reverted #757), scripts/sw-nav-check.mjs (NEW required-CI gate), scripts/boot-check.mjs (worker-controlled second pass), the DR-0157 re-land gate (DR-0160)
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE WORST OUTCOME, AGAIN, WITH A NEW FACE (Darrell 13:38Z, ERR_FAILED, "All PoeTech Apps Moore and Mines"): #750 merged at 13:30:45Z rewrote the worker to cache-first navigation; within 8 minutes every installed device on the origin was dead — while CI, the deploy run, verify-boot, and site-health ALL stayed green, because a fresh browser's FIRST visit is never service-worker-controlled and no instrument ever executed the broken fetch handler. ROOT (reproduced locally, both directions): install's cache.add follows Cloudflare Pages' /index.html→/ 301 and stores a `redirected` response; the cache-first path hands it to a navigation; a browser refuses a redirected response for a navigation → net::ERR_FAILED — the exact browser rule the worker's own 2026-07-07 REDIRECT GUARD comment documents, bypassed by the new cached path. RESTORED per DR-0107: revert to last-known-good network-first (#757, deploy 29098267913 green 14:01:57Z); the heal was PROVEN before being promised — sw.js script fetches bypass the broken handler and production serves sw.js no-store, so one visit swaps the worker on a stuck device (reproduced: broken profile + fixed serve → first reopen healed). STRUCTURAL CLOSE: sw-nav-check in the REQUIRED CI job (Cloudflare-shaped serve, two visits, control REQUIRED before judging — no vacuous green) + boot-check's second worker-controlled pass on the real domain each deploy. WAYS, TWICE: (1) a gate must stand where the USER stands — first visit vs Nth visit is a different machine; (2) BOTH new gates' first drafts passed the broken worker (one never registered a worker, one closed the page mid-check via a bare return before finally) — theater caught only because each gate was run against the known-broken artifact BEFORE being trusted; a gate ships with its CATCH demonstrated, never its green. CONSTRAINT (held): ~15-25s added to required CI. ROUTED: DR-0157's stay-on-previous-build goal re-lands un-redirected + gated, re-review 2026-07-17; a pure unit pin on "navigation responses are never redirected" rides the same date.
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0160-the-worker-is-gated-where-the-devices-stand.md
### REV-0048 · Post-feature O&C — push-to-end voice: a pause is not a Stop
- **Date:** 2026-07-10
- **Surface:** lib/voice-dictation.js — the ONE voice primitive under every input surface (OneVoiceInput/Yahweh Hears You, Thinking Space, and every future mic)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DEFECT (Darrell 2026-07-10: "Voice notes don't take long voice notes well... long pauses stop it instead of push to end for those inputs or all why not?"): the shared hook ran the browser recognizer with continuous=false and accepted the engine's silence-triggered end as the speaker's end — a thoughtful pause silently killed the note. THE FIX, once for ALL surfaces (the DR-0131 one-primitive dividend answers "why not all" — there is only one mic, so every input gets it): (1) PUSH-TO-END — the speaker's Stop tap is the only normal end; while the mic is held, an engine that quits on a pause is transparently restarted and the transcript keeps appending; (2) pause-shaped engine errors ('no-speech'/'aborted') are not errors mid-session; (3) THE BRAKE — a 5-minute hard session cap (VOICE_SESSION_CAP_MS) so a forgotten live microphone can never listen forever; on cap it stops with an honest message ("everything you said is kept"), never silently — the three-brakes posture applied to a mic; (4) duplicate-proof — continuous engines re-report the whole result list, so extractNewFinalTranscript forwards only new-and-final results; a pause never re-inserts the sentence before it. The pause-vs-stop decision is a PURE function (decideOnEngineEnd) pinned proven-to-catch: the old behavior fails the new tests. CONSTRAINTS (named honestly): the Web Speech engine is the browser's own (on Android/Chrome the recognition runs through the platform's speech service — acceptable for this surface today; the sovereign Whisper path remains the long-arc answer for fully-local transcription); iOS Safari support is partial and the mic hides where unsupported (type-only stays whole); auto-restart introduces a sub-second seam at each pause in which a word can drop on some engines — the seam is inherent to restart-based continuity. OPPORTUNITIES (routed): a visible "listening — Ns" elapsed indicator + a gentle pre-cap warning at 4:30, re-review 2026-07-22; sovereign Whisper dictation as the engine behind the same hook (NAS/GPU lane), re-review 2026-07-22; interimResults=true for live words-as-you-speak preview on capable engines, re-review 2026-07-22.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/voice-dictation.js

### REV-0049 · Harvest — L18 ships: the flinch comes first, and the Word had the order all along
- **Date:** 2026-07-11
- **Surface:** Living Lessons L18 ("The Flinch Comes First (body first, recognition second, choice third)" — the Dr. Tracey Marks harvest)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE HARVEST LANDS ON DOCTRINE THE PLATFORM ALREADY OWNS: Dr. Marks' fast-vs-slow processing sequence — the body's alarm ("the flinch") fires before meaning arrives; separate the flinch from the meaning; reset on "what do I actually know right now?"; create space with "let me think about that" — is the neuroscience underneath the Mind of Christ discipline (NOTICE → TEST → CAPTURE → REDIRECT), and the lesson says so explicitly. Elijah carries the whole arc (1 Kings 19:3-8): the body fled first, the false story came second under the juniper tree, and Yahweh ministered BODY-FIRST — food and sleep, twice, before the still small voice corrected the story — heaven's own pastoral care honoring the design. (3) transcript-scraper MCP \u2014 fragile/ToS-gray, lower clarity, not primary. Adoption gated on evidence from real use (DR-0143), never on popularity. Every verse fetched verbatim.

### REV-0059 · Harvest (capstone) — L24 "Yahweh Is the Only Way": the exclusivity proven from the uniqueness, data-driven and Word-first
- **Date:** 2026-07-11
- **Surface:** Living Lessons L24 ("Yahweh Is the Only Way (the case, from what is known)")
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE SUMMIT OF THE DAY'S HARVEST. After weighing every voice (L18-L23, World Issues 5-6), the capstone is the Godhead's exclusive claim, built data-driven per Darrell's framing ("past present and future at the same time, the only Being that can read everything and everybody, and as non-body made all the worlds"). The EXCLUSIVITY is grounded in the UNIQUENESS, laid out as three attributes no rival holds: ETERNAL PRESENT (Exodus 3:14 "I AM THAT I AM"; Revelation 1:8; Psalm 90:2; Malachi 3:6) - which is why prophecy is His signature (Isaiah 46:10 "declaring the end from the beginning"); OMNISCIENCE reads-every-heart (1 Chronicles 28:9; Hebrews 4:13; Jeremiah 23:24; Psalm 147:5 - the mind-reading Scripture grants Yahweh ALONE, the platform's ACCESS-TO-THE-HUMAN-MIND foundation); and SPIRIT-CREATOR-of-all (John 4:24; Isaiah 44:24 "alone... by myself"; Colossians 1:16-17; Hebrews 11:3; Romans 1:20 - the 4th-dimensional WORD-FIRST frame of CLAUDE.md). The DATA stated at the confidence known (DR-0100): fulfilled prophecy demonstrably pre-dated (Micah 5:2 Bethlehem; Isaiah 53 with the ~125 BC Great Isaiah Scroll; Psalm 22:16,18 pierced hands/feet ~1000 BC; Zechariah 11:12-13 thirty pieces to the potter) and the resurrection offered as FALSIFIABLE data (1 Corinthians 15:3-6 - named witnesses, most still living, "go ask them"). The logic of ONE Way as MERCY not miserliness (Romans 6:23; Hebrews 9:22; 1 Peter 1:18-19; John 3:16), "many paths" TAUGHT PAST not debated (DR-0098 - contradictory exclusive claims cannot all be true; the verdict spoken, Acts 4:12), and the invitation held as wide as the Word (Isaiah 45:22 "all the ends of the earth"; Revelation 22:17 "whosoever will"; Romans 10:9; faith as real-ground step, Hebrews 11:1). Godhead precision kept: Yahweh the Father is the one God (Deuteronomy 6:4), the one way to Him is the Son by the Spirit (John 14:6). ~30 verse-sets fetched verbatim. Series 23 -> 24. OPPORTUNITY (routed): a Godhead-Study cross-link (gh-door-christ already exists) and a Discernment-track tie (this is the positive counterpart to the falsified-claims work) - rides the 2026-07-22 cross-link re-review.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js

### REV-0060 · Harvest — L25 "The Threshold and the Two Patterns": biblical patterns taught, speculative taxonomy bounded
- **Date:** 2026-07-11
- **Surface:** Living Lessons L25 ("The Threshold and the Two Patterns" — the Taylor Welch transition/leadership harvest)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE DISCERNMENT POSTURE APPLIED TO CHRISTIAN TEACHING (not only secular): the harvest's genuine biblical substance is taught firmly while the speculative demon-taxonomy is bounded per DR-0098 + the reticence rule (ACCESS-TO-THE-HUMAN-MIND: "anyone claiming a precise schema of spirit-to-mind transmission has gone beyond what is written"). TAUGHT AS REAL BIBLICAL PATTERNS: the transition/threshold reframe (discomfort is often the new season's unfamiliar rules, not an attack — Luke 22:31-32, Peter's permitted sifting answered by intercession not frantic warfare); NIMROD = the self-made name (Genesis 10:8-9; 11:4 "make us a name" — the religion of human potential); JEZEBEL = control-by-manipulation (1 Kings 21:25; Revelation 2:20 the seducing false-prophetess type); the PERMISSION mechanism (Ephesians 4:27 "neither give place"; 2 Corinthians 10:4-5 strongholds built by agreed-with thoughts, pulled down by captive thoughts — the platform's own mind foundation: adversary works by suggestion, needs partnership, cannot force the will); endure-and-surrender in the RIGHT order (James 4:6-7 submit-THEN-resist, God resists the proud; 1 Peter 5:6 humble-and-wait, He exalts in due time — antidote to both name-building and control); IDENTITY OVER PERFORMANCE as the root-cut (Ephesians 2:8-10 His workmanship by grace not works; Galatians 2:20 Christ living in me); three guards (fruit-inspect self, 2 Corinthians 13:5 + Matthew 7:16-20; trusted community, Proverbs 27:17 + Ecclesiastes 4:9-10; surrender the grip + pray for those resented, Matthew 5:44); endure without weariness (Isaiah 40:31; Galatians 6:9). BOUNDED HONESTLY (stated in the lesson itself, both levels + talking points): "territorial governors stationed at thresholds" and a defined "spirit of Nimrod/Jezebel" entity-class are interpretive LENS, not chapter-and-verse — the Word is real about the unseen realm (Ephesians 6:12; Daniel 10:13) and sparing about its mechanics; teach the pattern, hold the schema loosely, keep it pastoral not sensational (no demon-hunting). Connects to MIND-OF-CHRIST (captive thoughts), L18 (the flinch), L19 (influence). ~19 verse-sets fetched verbatim. Series 24 -> 25. OPPORTUNITY (routed): a cross-link to the MIND-OF-CHRIST discipline and the L19 influence-shield rides the 2026-07-22 cross-link re-review.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js

SHIPPED: a benefit bullet naming the two courts added to ALL 6 discernment lessons (the visible summary the reader sees, not only the deep lens); a deterministic gate (accountability/eternal-court-not-in-benefits) requiring it — SCOPED to hasDocumented so a lesson not about documented wrongdoing is never forced to carry it, honoring Darrell's exact caveat ("unless it's not about that subject or doesn't discuss it"). Proven-to-catch: three tests — fires when the benefit is missing, passes when present, and confirms it stays SILENT with no documented harm. Verified earlier the same session that all 6 lessons already carried accountability + the eternal element in their deep lens; this pass makes it visible + enforced. Every verse fetched verbatim. Full suite 5376 green; build clean.

Built as ONE Living Lesson with two movements of God's knowing: (1) COVENANTAL -- the King documented His Ways (Deuteronomy 29:29; Psalm 119) and made His people a royal priesthood to do them (Revelation 1:6; 1 Peter 2:9); the knowing runs both ways and is proven by obedience (Exodus 33:13 "shew me thy way that I may know thee"; 1 John 2:3-4; John 10:14; 1 Corinthians 8:3), guarded by the sobering inverse aimed at the gifted-and-busy (Matthew 7:21-23 "I never knew you, ye that work iniquity"); and the royal honour is to SEARCH OUT His mysteries (Proverbs 25:2 -- the crown verse; Jeremiah 33:3; 1 Corinthians 2:7-10 the Spirit searches the deep things); (2) ETERNAL -- chosen in Him BEFORE the foundation of the world (Ephesians 1:4), the golden chain foreknew/predestinated/called/justified/glorified (Romans 8:29-30), grace given before the world began not by works (2 Timothy 1:9), the "amazing details" of the members written in His book before fashioned (Psalm 139:15-16; 1 Peter 1:2; John 15:16). THE HINGE: 2 Timothy 2:19 holds both in one seal -- "The Lord knoweth them that are his" (eternal, rest) AND "let every one that nameth the name of Christ depart from iniquity" (covenantal, live) -- election fuels obedience, never excuses it. Light resonance noted without making it the point: the King documents His Ways so His kings do His will -- the same reason this house documents its Ways. ~19 verse-sets fetched verbatim. Series 25 -> 26. OPPORTUNITY (routed): a Godhead-Study cross-link (foreknowledge/election) and a tie to L24 (the only Way) ride the 2026-07-22 cross-link re-review.

So like Him I look at my childhood as a garden of Eden and derive lessons from the biblical Scriptures to relate to Yahweh and the Godhead -- find all relationships and create lessons." Built as ONE Living Lesson with two movements: (1) THE GODHEAD DOCUMENTED HIS OWN GRIEF so we would understand and BELIEVE Him (DR-0166 believe-first) -- the Father grieved (Genesis 6:6 "it grieved him at his heart"; the grieving search "Where art thou?" 3:9; Hosea 11:8 "mine heart is turned within me"; Isaiah 63:9 "in all their affliction he was afflicted"), the incarnate Son "a man of sorrows, and acquainted with grief" (Isaiah 53:3) who wept at a grave (John 11:35), over a city (Luke 19:41), and in Gethsemane (Matthew 26:38), the Spirit grieved (Ephesians 4:30) -- the whole Godhead documented FEELING; (2) HE RECORDS OUR TEARS TOO and teaches us the JOSEPH METHOD of reading our own story by His Word -- "put thou my tears into thy bottle: are they not in thy book?" (Psalm 56:8, the gold anchor); Joseph names the real evil but reads it under God's hand ("ye thought evil against me; but God meant it unto good... to preserve life" Genesis 50:20; 45:5); the three questions the Scriptures answer (where was God -- Isaiah 63:9; what was He preserving/preparing -- Deuteronomy 8:2; what comfort can I now give -- 2 Corinthians 1:3-4), remembered on purpose (Psalm 77:11) under mercy "new every morning" (Lamentations 3:22-23), "all things work together for good" (Romans 8:28), toward the settled end "God shall wipe away all tears" (Revelation 21:4). TLC BRIGHT LINE HELD: framed as reflection WITH the Godhead through His Word, NOT clinical therapy; an explicit pastoral guardrail names that deep wounds welcome a trusted person/pastor alongside (wisdom, not weakness). Word-first: the lesson builds the UNIVERSAL method; a person's SPECIFIC childhood memories are theirs to bring (not fabricated -- DR-0076). ~18 verse-sets fetched verbatim from app/public/bible/kjv. Series 26 -> 27. Full suite 5382 green; lint clean; build clean.

### REV-0066 · Feature — Ari's comprehensive, cloud-runnable app review
- **Date:** 2026-07-11
- **Surface:** Projects → "Ari Review" sub-tab; lib/ari-app-review.js + components/AriReview.jsx
- **Type:** code-review
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** DARRELL'S ASK (2026-07-11, two Projects screenshots): "We need Ari to have better comprehensive reviews of the PoeTech App." REALITY-TRACE FIRST (DR-0061): the house had review PIECES — Perpetual Report (history), the NAS-bound LLM diff review (needs NAS + a branch), Concerns, App Firm-Up's single %, the board/build sync — but no ONE cloud-runnable review that ASSESSES the whole app across dimensions with evidence per finding. The screenshots pointed straight at the gap ("11 open items undated"; "10 items the build record marks shipped still read Not started"). SHIPPED (DR-0175): Ari Review — a ranked, dimensional health read synthesized from the app's own REAL records across five dimensions: (1) delivery integrity — board vs the build record (staleSeedStatuses = the exact shipped-but-Not-started drift; missingSeedTasks = unloaded specs, only on boards in use so a new user's empty world is never flagged); (2) plan health — undated open items (can't reach the timeline, DR-0170) + overdue open items; (3) review freshness — dated re-reviews parsed from REVIEWS.md + the DR ledger that are overdue/soon; (4) concern & feedback backlog; (5) data integrity — deriveDataConcerns contradictions. TRUSTWORTHY BY CONSTRUCTION (DR-0076): every finding carries its real COUNT + SOURCE + the existing fix action (evidence, not claims); a clean dimension reads "clear", never a painted score; it COMPOSES the already-tested signal producers rather than re-deriving them; deterministic (nowMs injected) + proven-to-catch — each dimension fires on a real contradiction and stays silent when clean, and the drift fixture is pinned against the real seed spec (bt-seed-board-modular-cutover-s0 status 'done'). Advisory, not a gate — the CI gates stay the merge brake (GOVERN-EXECUTE-ADVISE). COLOR THEOLOGY held (DR-0099): severities use brick/terracotta/olive/muted, never true red. New Ari standing duty `comprehensive-review`. 12 new tests; full suite 5416 green; lint + build clean; legibility PASS (new page 170, no new dark-on-dark).
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0175-ari-comprehensive-app-review.md

### REV-0067 · Ways-review — the ministry-support spine (bus/van), 1:1 messaging, and sovereign meetings
- **Date:** 2026-07-12
- **Surface:** Church → Bus Ministry sub-tab; migrations 0095/0096/0097; libs bus-ministry(.js/-sync.js), direct-messages(.js/-sync.js), ministry-meetings(.js/-sync.js); components BusMinistry.jsx + DirectMessages.jsx + SecurityPanel.jsx; docs MINISTRY-SUPPORT-PATTERN.md + SOVEREIGN-COMMS-AND-MEETINGS.md
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** DEACON ANDERSON'S ASK + DARRELL'S EXPANSION, SHIPPED SAME SESSION (2026-07-12). The live failure named on the call was the reminder that never went out ("they're not calling my drivers... no one's on the phones... once the schedule come out, the reminders need to go [out]"). REALITY-TRACE FIRST (DR-0061): the Choir module (0011) already proved the exact shape a ministry needs — roster + schedule + reminders + shared thread — so the bus ministry is that spine RE-SKINNED, not a new architecture (this is the WAYS finding: a proven module is a template; the 2nd instance reuses the 1st's tables + user_in_X() + makeSubscriber + deriveAccess + testable-pure-logic, it does not re-architect — which is why the whole build took one session). Built the six-primitive spine (0095): roster w/ phone+email, weekly Sunday schedule w/ driver self-confirm, the Thursday-before reminder queue (the exact "nobody called them" fix), shared thread, and a dev/ops intake (bus_requests) so the ministry hands new requirements to the build team without a call (the streamlined loop, DR-0103, extended to ministries). DARRELL'S EXPANSION handled in the same session: 1:1 direct messages + roster-to-roster + report-to-security (0096, DR-0181) with a participant-only-read RLS model and users_can_dm (leader->anyone, roster->roster like usher->security, conservative-by-construction for minors); and "our own OBS-based Zoom/Teams" (0097, DR-0182) — the scheduling shell + load rules (the three brakes applied to meetings: <=25 people / <=180 min / <=3 concurrent, one meeting per ministry, real start required, all proven-to-catch) built now, the real-time OBS video engine captured HONESTLY as the Tier-C target rather than painting a fake "join call" (reality-trace). VERIFICATION (DR-0076): 47 new pure-logic tests are the gate (bus 25 + DM 8 + meetings 14); every cited verse (Galatians 6:2, Ephesians 4:29, Matthew 18:15, Colossians 4:6, Luke 14:28, 1 Cor 14:40) is KJV-verbatim from in-repo data. HONEST LIMITS STATED (DR-0100): reminders are in-app + coordinator-logged, not yet auto-SMS; account-linking is the gating dependency for driver self-confirm/DM; security<->Observation is data-connected but not yet surfaced together; the OBS video engine is not built. Tripped + fixed 4 baseline guards from the new surfaces (emoji-as-icon -> text, church-bus feedback area registered, 2 backgrounds swapped to covered midnight tokens, legibility health/baseline regenerated). Full suite 5510 green; lint + build clean; all CI guards pass (boundary, monolith-budget, interconnect, source-adapter, surface-audit, contrast, legibility, consistency, feedback-area).
- **Source:** docs/decisions/DR-0180-bus-ministry-and-the-ministry-support-pattern.md

### REV-0070 · Orchestration — the autonomous operating model researched, documented, and implemented

WAYS-REVIEW (DR-0108) executed: (1) RESEARCHED the established patterns — IBM autonomic computing's MAPE-K reference loop (Monitor->Analyze->Plan->Execute over shared Knowledge; closed-loop self-management on the homeostasis analogy) and autonomous business-process automation (exception-based management: the machine handles volume, humans handle exceptions; human-on-the-loop for high-stakes; a measured straight-through rate; graduated rollout — start supervised, widen as reliability is measured). (2) DOCUMENTED as the Ways: AUTONOMOUS-OPERATING-MODEL.md names the model and maps it 1:1 onto pieces already built (Monitor+Analyze = ari-app-review DR-0175; Plan = the ari-adjustments gate; Execute = gated auto-apply + the human exception path; Knowledge = the audit log). Recorded as DR-0185; grounds the DR-0111/DR-0178 through-line (motion is the default; a question is an exception for a genuine bright line only) as the operating default. (3) IMPLEMENTED after testing: ari-loop.js composes the review + gate into one MEASURED loop (STP rate = auto/(auto+propose); the graduated-autonomy stage; the five MAPE-K stages), surfaced in Ari Review. VERIFICATION (DR-0076): the gate NEVER auto-applies money/PHI/published changes and the loop reports a REAL straight-through rate, never a painted 100% — proven-to-catch in ari-adjustments.test.js (11) + ari-loop.test.js (7). HONEST FRONTIER (DR-0100): this ships the decision gate + audit log + measured loop; the per-surface safe-op executors that let Execute actually perform each fix are the named next increment (each raises the measured STP), not painted as done. Advisory — CI gates stay the merge brake. Lint + full suite green.

### REV-0071 · Harvest — L28 "Prove All Things": search out a matter, and the tool that tells you to test it
- **Date:** 2026-07-15
- **Surface:** Living Lessons L28 ("Prove All Things"); app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** DARRELL BROUGHT A VIDEO INTO THE APP (2026-07-15) that used A.I. analysis to test the century-old attribution of the Great Sphinx of Giza to Pharaoh Khafre and found the evidence thinner than the textbooks imply (no inscription names the builder; a 2026 A.I. facial study came back INCONCLUSIVE, echoing forensic artist Frank Domingo's earlier proportional doubts; a recarving suggestion; a contested water-erosion age claim; and the "Napoleon shot the nose" tale debunked by sketches that predate Napoleon). Captured as a Word-first Living Lesson whose point is the METHOD, not a verdict on the Sphinx. FIVE MOVEMENTS: (1) PROVING IS COMMANDED, not rebellious -- "prove all things; hold fast that which is good" (1 Thessalonians 5:21); the Bereans were "more noble" for verifying even Paul (Acts 17:11); "try the spirits" (1 John 4:1). (2) THE SEARCH IS ROYAL -- "the honour of kings is to search out a matter" (Proverbs 25:2); "he that is first in his own cause seemeth just; but his neighbour cometh and searcheth him" (Proverbs 18:17; 18:13). (3) THE WORKED EXAMPLE held in the DR-0100 three tiers -- state the established plainly (the nose myth predates Napoleon), flag the genuinely-open NARROWLY (the age/identity), overclaim NEITHER direction (not "it's Khafre," not "it's older" -- both would be trading one overconfident story for another). (4) THE TOOL THAT TELLS YOU TO TEST IT -- the A.I.'s "inconclusive" was the HONEST answer, which is exactly Ari's binding honesty ("he can be wrong -- test what matters," ari.js); A.I. is a lamp UNDER the Word, never over or in place of it (Colossians 2:8; WORD-FIRST DR-0098 -- consensus gets no veto and no automatic win). (5) THE GOD WHO SEES -- our sight is partial (1 Corinthians 13:12), His is total (Job 12:22; Luke 8:17; Mark 4:22); "Thou God seest me" (Genesis 16:13, El Roi -- Ari's own unseen-made-seen heart); "the truth shall make you free" (John 8:32). VERIFICATION (DR-0076): every verse KJV-verbatim from app/public/bible/kjv; the archaeology figures/claims are attributed as the VIDEO's, NOT asserted by the house as fact. Series 27 -> 28; META weeks 27 -> 28. lesson-flow + learn-catalog-render + ari tests green (34); lint clean; monolith-budget/interconnect/source-adapter/legibility/surface-audit PASS; build clean.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/living-lessons-class.js

CORE TAUGHT PLAINLY: (1) fruit is the BYPRODUCT of abiding, not a work -- "he that abideth in me... bringeth forth much fruit: for without me ye can do nothing" (John 15:5); so strange fruit is a SYMPTOM of a root (Matthew 12:33; 3:10). (3) the EMPTY house refills "seven times worse" unless FILLED (Matthew 12:43-45) -- fill with the Father (Psalm 68:5; Romans 8:15; John 14:18), truth over lies (2 Corinthians 10:4-5; 4:2), the Spirit (Ephesians 5:18), re-rooted (Colossians 2:7). (5) deliverance REQUIRES discipleship -- Lazarus raised but bound, "Loose him, and let him go" (John 11:44); restore in meekness (Galatians 6:1); minister healed first (Matthew 7:5). (6) abide vs survive; life "more abundantly" (John 10:10); whole person (1 Thessalonians 5:23; 3 John 2). PRACTICAL SELF-ASSESSMENT woven in biblically (trace fruit->root: when/what-trigger/what-lie; renounce + re-root; watch UNDER PRESSURE where the root shows -- Luke 6:45; 2 Corinthians 13:5). DISCERNMENT/PASTORAL DISCIPLINE (load-bearing, ACCESS-TO-THE-HUMAN-MIND + TLC bright line): the guest's "agreement with spirits / break the agreement" framing presented as HIS language, grounded in durable truth (yield->serve, Romans 6:16), but the house bound kept EXPLICIT throughout -- NOT every strange fruit is a demon; Scripture names "the works of the flesh" (Galatians 5:19) and "out of the heart" (Mark 7:21), much is real wound + body (cross-ref Made in Time trauma/body lessons); safe primary work = abide, find root, fill void, in community -- NO demon-hunting; deep wounds -> a trusted pastor/professional (wisdom, not weakness). - **Findings:** DARRELL asked "Are we making sure to use new data to upgrade and/or start another lesson?" and chose BOTH: adopt a standing per-teaching routing rule AND do a cross-reference pass now. THE STANDING RULE (grounds DR-0075 perpetual-improvement + Darrell's earlier Heiser-era instruction "cross reference... add it to those courses if it gives more clarity and weight... unless it needs its own lesson"): for each NEW teaching fed in, state the routing explicitly up front -- UPGRADE an existing lesson (fold the data in) vs START a new lesson (distinct enough to stand alone) -- and, crucially, re-open even a MERGED lesson with a follow-up commit when new data genuinely SHARPENS it (not merely restates it; pure reiterations of already-captured material are correctly no-ops). Examples this session: upgrades folded pre-commit (Manolo resource/privacy -> L35; Halwell manufacturing/wood + self-assessment + pressure-test -> L36); new lessons where distinct (Kokotajlo -> L34; Manolo -> L35 as L34's constructive counterpart; Halwell -> L36); every new lesson cross-links to related ones. THE CROSS-REFERENCE PASS (make the web BIDIRECTIONAL where the "pairs with" convention already exists): (1) L34 "The Tower, the Race, and the Sovereign" now forward-links to its constructive companion L35 "Sovereign and Small" (L35 already pointed back -> AI pair complete). (2) Made in Time L10 "Wired to Survive" (trauma) now links to Living Lesson L36 "Fruit, Root, and the Empty House" (wound = the misdiagnosed root; heal by filling the void) -- L36 already pointed back. Prose-only edits, no new verses, no new lessons, no META/schema change. Full suite green; lint + build + catalog-render clean.

### REV-0088 · Feature — token invite + guardian re-confirm (DR-0187, closes the bare-email self-claim gap)
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** infra/supabase/migrations-auto/0104-token-invite-guardian-reconfirm.sql; app/src/lib/family-invite.js; app/src/components/FamilyInvitePanel.jsx; app/src/components/ClaimInviteBanner.jsx (new); app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (mount)
- **Type:** security
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** DARRELL directed the build of DR-0187 leg 1. Closes the gap the research found: join_default_instance (0081) granted an invited role to ANYONE signing in with a matching email string, while email-confirmation ships OFF — so knowing an invited email was enough. NEW two-party handshake, NO external channel required (built for the uncle whose Gmail is locked): (1) invite_to_instance now mints a one-time CLAIM TOKEN and returns {id,token,email,role}; the guardian DELIVERS the link (poetech.us/?join=<token>) however they reach the person ("DMs not SMS"). (2) claim_invite(token) records a PENDING claim only (grants nothing). (3) list_pending_claims() surfaces claims awaiting the inviting owner/admin. (4) confirm_invite(invite_id) — the guardian re-confirms; ONLY THEN is membership inserted. join_default_instance's bare-email branch (b) REMOVED; membership now comes only from a confirmed claim. SAFE BY CONSTRUCTION: the "already a member" guard + founder allowlist (bootstrap seed; tenancy-guard stays green) are UNCHANGED — no existing member affected; only the not-yet-member invite path changed. UI: FamilyInvitePanel now shows the deliverable link per invite (copy) + an "Approve who claimed" re-confirm section; ClaimInviteBanner (new, self-contained, fail-soft — renders NOTHING without a ?join token) is the invitee's claim entry, mounted after AuthBanner. TESTS (proven-to-catch, DR-0076): family-invite.test.js +6 (buildClaimLink encodes token, readClaimTokenFromUrl round-trips + never throws); family-invite-panel-render updated to the two-party posture; claim-invite-banner-render (new) pins "renders nothing for a normal visitor" + surfaces the prompt with a token. Full suite + lint + build + legibility (health regenerated) + contrast + consistency green. TWO-HALF DELIVERY (sovereign infra): the app code ships the normal lane; the SQL migration 0104 must be APPLIED ON THE NAS Supabase (Darrell, via ConnectBot/Studio) for the feature to activate — paste-ready steps provided. Tier C — reviewed live before trusting with real people (DR-0104). Follow-ups per DR-0187: steward provision-and-reset (leg 2), optional email OTP (leg 3), phone as-is; SMS deferred to a money-decision.

### REV-0092 · Harvest — Living Lesson L38: how the house teaches WELL (Myron Golden), seek/do/teach → four rungs → become the message
- **Date:** 2026-07-18

- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged

NAMED the teacher; kept the WORD SENIOR to the communication method (guardrail stated in the provenance comment + facilitator): the aim is to serve, never to use an audience to validate self. Golden's frame mapped onto Scripture's own: (1) the ORDER = Ezra 7:10 verbatim — "prepared his heart to SEEK the law... and to DO it, and to TEACH"; research to LEARN and LIVE, not merely lecture — "be ye doers of the word, and not hearers only" (James 1:22); lived truth → "become the message," "ye are our epistle... known and read of all men" (2 Corinthians 3:2). (2) FOUR RUNGS lowest→highest: TACTICS (what — necessary, never sufficient; knowing ≠ doing) → STRATEGIES (when/why — sequence + reason) → PRINCIPLES (God's automation, universal laws — "whatsoever a man soweth, that shall he also reap," Galatians 6:7) → ESSENCE (the character/nature of God; operate from IDENTITY not imitation — "we have the mind of Christ," 1 Corinthians 2:16; "not I, but Christ liveth in me," Galatians 2:20; "ambassadors for Christ," 2 Corinthians 5:20). (3) INTEGRITY the foundation — God "cannot lie" (Titus 1:2; Hebrews 6:18; Numbers 23:19); tell truth because it's the only thing worth telling; keep your word when it costs — "sweareth to his own hurt, and changeth not" (Psalm 15:4); covenant (backed by existence) not contract (mutual distrust). (4) CLARITY — the message must LAND (biggest problem in communication is assuming it happened): "if the trumpet give an uncertain sound, who shall prepare himself to the battle?" (1 Corinthians 14:8); words "easy to be understood," else "speak into the air" (14:9); "God is not the author of confusion, but of peace" (14:33). (5) WORD PICTURES the Lord's own tool — "without a parable spake he not unto them" (Matthew 13:34); "as they were able to hear it" (Mark 4:33) — impact over impression. (6) the AIM keeps it holy — teach to SERVE: "he that is greatest among you shall be your servant" (Matthew 23:11); "not... lords over God's heritage, but... ensamples to the flock" (1 Peter 5:3); "a pattern of good works... sound speech, that cannot be condemned" (Titus 2:7-8). Cross-links "Beholding Him" (mit17) via 2 Corinthians 3:18 (behold→become, then you have something real to teach). Every verse KJV-verbatim; 21 fragments machine-spot-checked against public/bible/kjv (DR-0076). Full shape check (38 modules, all keys, valid quiz indices), lint, catalog-render + learn-framework + learn-integration tests (29 passed), and production build all green.

### REV-0093 · Ways-review (orchestration) — I guessed a manual migration-apply path instead of using our db-migrate auto-lane (DR-0108)
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** how-we-work (migration apply path); memory `project_db_migrations_auto_lane`
- **Type:** orchestration
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** THE MISS (a repeat of a documented one): applying migration 0104 (token-invite/guardian-reconfirm, DR-0187), I handed Darrell a manual apply path — first NAS `docker exec ... psql` steps via ConnectBot, then, after his `docker ps` showed only n8n/ntfy/ollama (no Supabase container), a cloud Supabase SQL-editor paste. BOTH were wrong. THE VERIFIED REALITY: (a) production DB is Supabase CLOUD (`mjjlevhdufpaplypnqrv.supabase.co`), NOT the NAS — the NAS self-host is PAUSED/never-came-up (`docs/PROJECT-FRAMEWORK.md:316`), which is exactly why no supabase container runs there; (b) far more important, migrations APPLY THEMSELVES — any idempotent `.sql` under `infra/supabase/migrations-auto/` is run against the cloud DB by the `db-migrate` GitHub Actions lane (`.github/workflows/db-migrate.yml` → `scripts/db-migrate-apply.sh`, `SUPABASE_DB_URL` secret) on merge to main. MERGE = MIGRATE. This is documented verbatim in memory `project_db_migrations_auto_lane` ("never hand Darrell SQL to paste, never guess docker / Studio / psql") — the SAME failure that cost him time on the `0074` migration ("I don't have that... you fix it first, why me"). I did not consult the memory/ways before improvising, so I repeated it. PROVEN OUTCOME (DR-0076): on the #891 merge the db-migrate lane auto-applied 0104 to cloud — log shows `=== applying 0104-... ===` → ALTER TABLE / UPDATE 3 / CREATE INDEX / DROP FUNCTION / CREATE FUNCTION ×5 / GRANT ×5 → `summary: applied=1 skipped=116 failed=0`; all four RPCs (invite_to_instance, claim_invite, list_pending_claims, confirm_invite) + the join_default_instance replacement are live. Darrell had to do NOTHING. THE FORWARD FIX (ways): before handing ANY manual command to the team, first check whether an established lane already does it — the WAYS-REVIEW question "what tool/capability does the TEAM/system already have that I am not using?" applies to our OWN automation, not just Darrell's tools. For DB/SQL specifically: put the SQL in a `migrations-auto/*.sql` file, let the lane run it on merge, and read the run log / `_schema_migrations` ledger for the receipt — never improvise a manual apply path. Also flags the stale NAS-Supabase-Studio belief (carried in the session CLAUDE.md dispatch note + older 99-session-notes) as superseded: the live DB is cloud + auto-lane per `project_db_migrations_auto_lane`. Governed by DR-0108; pairs with feedback-surface-premise-conflicts + VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE.
- **Source:** .github/workflows/db-migrate.yml

Captured as a NEW discernment Living Lesson L39 (the L37 "One Blood" three-tier engine applied to biblical GEOGRAPHY and CHRISTOLOGY). The claim-set: (a) the true Jerusalem/Zion is in sub-Saharan Africa and Rome/Helena re-mapped the Levant; (b) the Ebionites were the unbroken original church and the Godhead/Trinity is a pagan Roman add-on; (c) the Pharisees doctored the LXX-vs-Masoretic timeline to bury history. HANDLED per DR-0098/DR-0100/DR-0076 + the source-conflict rule: HONOR the real grain (Africa's genuine honored place — Ps 68:31; Acts 8:27; Acts 13:1; Num 12:1; the Hebrew roots — John 4:22; Rom 11:18; real LXX/MT/Samaritan variants incl. Luke 3:36 "Cainan"); HOLD the Godhead as the line that never moves — Scripture's OWN, pre-Nicene (John 1:1,14; 8:58; 10:30; 20:28; Col 2:9; Col 1:16-17; Isa 9:6; Matt 28:19; Acts 5:4; James 2:1 vs the Ebionites' actual low christology + Paul-rejection); ANSWER the geography by the Word's map (exile EAST to Babylon, 2 Kings 25:11; "down from Jerusalem to Jericho," Luke 10:30; "mountains round about," Ps 125:2; Gihon, 2 Chr 32:30; peacocks+gold IMPORTED by trade, 1 Kings 10:22; lion-of-Judah native, Rev 5:5; Josephus locates it in Judea); WEIGH the timeline narrowly (Abraham→Exodus FIXED at 430 yrs, Exodus 12:40 / Gal 3:17; years-before-Abraham ≠ a city's location; "prove all things," 1 Thess 5:21); DISCERN the device (rides a real grievance [whitewashing] + real grains into an unfalsifiable "they hid it," "an angel of light," 2 Cor 11:14; "we are not ignorant of his devices," 2 Cor 2:11; aim = pry the believer off the Godhead; "contend for the faith," Jude 3; "speaking the truth in love," Eph 4:15). ### REV-0099 · Fix (CRITICAL, family books) — imported transactions collapsed to a few rows/dates: the `t-${Date.now()}` id collision
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js; app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (addTransaction); app/src/__tests__/bulk-statement-import.test.js
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** CHRISTINA (Darrell's wife, on the family's REAL books) 2026-07-18: "the app correctly reports the exact number of transactions imported... however, once the import is complete, the transactions do not appear... it only displays transactions from about 3 to 5 different dates... the 5/26 date won't go away." Reality-traced (P15/P16) the import→commit→sync→display flow. ROOT CAUSE: both import loops — CSV (BooksTransactions.jsx:733) and bulk statement (:782) — call addTransaction in a SYNCHRONOUS forEach; addTransaction (shell:3131) fell back to `id: item.id || \`t-${Date.now()}\`` for rows without a fitid; in a tight loop Date.now() returns the SAME millisecond, so MANY imported rows got the SAME id. Consequences: cloud upsert keys on the id-slug → all same-id rows collapsed to ONE cloud row; mergeTransactionsPreferCloud then dropped the local duplicates (remoteIds.has) leaving the collapsed few; runningBalances' Map keyed by id also collapsed them. Net: count correct at parse, but only a handful survived (plus the seed 5/26 row, SEED_IDS) → "don't appear / only 3-5 dates." FIX: (1) bulk-statement-import.js now assigns EVERY planned row a stable unique id — fitid when present, else the content dedupe key `imp-<cKey>` (unique per distinct txn AND idempotent across re-imports via seedSeen); (2) addTransaction's fallback id is now collision-proof — `t-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}` (net-0 line, monolith budget held at 5447). PROVEN-TO-CATCH: new test imports 60 no-fitid rows and asserts all ids are defined + unique (undefined/duplicate ids fail it — the exact pre-fix break). bulk-import + statement-import + imported-view + dedupe tests (67 passed), lint, monolith-budget guard, production build all green. NOTE: this is a data-DISPLAY/sync-collapse fix; no data was destroyed on the bank side — re-import after deploy should now show all rows (idempotent, so safe to re-run).
- **Source:** app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js

### REV-0101 · Fix + Upgrade (family books) — balance-aware dedupe: genuine same-amount repeats survive; a balance-continuity audit proves the ledger
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/statement-import.js (capture Balance col) · app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js (txnDedupeKey +balance, persist balance) · app/src/lib/imported-view.js (auditBalanceContinuity)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Darrell asked for data-driven cleaning "so no two transactions are the same unless we paid twice the same amount... it should have metadata" — identity decided by the DATA, not by a human who could undermine it. Measured on Christina's REAL 1520-row Chase export: the old content dedupe key (accountId|date|amount|desc40) had 16 colliding keys and would WRONGLY DROP 17 legitimate repeat transactions (two $200 deposits 7/17, three $1.12 Auntie Anne's 7/13, two 6/1 ATM fees, etc.) as "duplicates" — a books-integrity defect. The disambiguating metadata already ships in the Chase export: the running Balance column (populated on 1502/1520 rows). Fix: (1) parser now captures the Balance column when present; (2) the dedupe key appends the running balance, so two genuine same-amount purchases get DIFFERENT keys (different running balance) and are BOTH kept, while a true re-import computes the SAME balance and still dedupes — content-derived, deterministic, idempotent, un-gameable; (3) new auditBalanceContinuity() is the quantitative "data validates data" engine — order-independent (never re-sorts, so same-day order can't false-alarm; integer cents so float drift can't fake a break): every row's balance-after must be consumed as exactly one other row's balance-before, leaving exactly one opening + one closing; a dropped or double-counted row splits the chain and the unmatched balances bracket the gap. Verified on the real file: NEW key keeps 1519 (was 1503 / 17 wrongly dropped), re-import is a 0-new no-op (idempotent), and the continuity audit PASSES on 1502 rows (opening $300.50 -> closing $1,007.88 — proven complete + un-double-counted). Proven-to-catch tests added: two same-amount repeats survive by balance; true re-import dedupes; audit passes a clean chain (incl. a genuine repeat) and CATCHES both a dropped and a double-counted row; float-safe; honest "not enough balance data" when absent. bulk-statement-import (12) + imported-view (38) + statement-import (20) = 70 passed. Also recommended: import OFX/QFX when the bank offers it — it carries the bank's authoritative FITID (already honored), the gold-standard unique id.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js

The family needs to reset one account and re-import clean, and that must be THEIR own gated action (DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT: the family governs their data; the destructive click is theirs, never the agent's server-side). Added: (1) accountTxnIds(txns, accountId) — pure, returns the ids of ONLY the chosen account's transactions, so a reset can never reach another account's ledger; (2) clearAccountRegister() in the import modal — a plain confirm names the EXACT count + account and states it's irreversible (incl. manual entries in that account), then loops deleteTransaction; (3) an inline "reset this account & re-import clean (N in it now)" control under the Target-account selector, showing the live count. Proven-to-catch tests: accountTxnIds returns only the target account's ids (never another account's), skips id-less rows, and is empty for no-account/empty-ledger (nothing cleared by accident). bulk-statement-import 14 passed; lint clean; production build green. This is the transition tool for Christina: reset the checking account, then re-import the clean Chase statement — the REV-0101 dedupe keeps every genuine repeat and the continuity audit will confirm the register ties out.
- **Source:** app/src/components/BooksTransactions.jsx

### REV-0103 · Fix (family books) — recover truly-identical PENDING twins by occurrence index; validated end-to-end on the real file (1520/1520)
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js (seedSeen + planBulkImport occurrence index)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** End-to-end validation on Christina's REAL uploaded 1520-row Chase file (Darrell: "she already uploaded it... Test? validate that it works?") surfaced ONE remaining wrongly-dropped transaction: TWO genuine $200 ONLINE DEPOSIT line items on 7/17, both PENDING (no running balance posted yet) and no FITID — identical in every field the dedupe key uses, so the balance-aware key (REV-0101) still collapsed them to one. Balance disambiguates POSTED rows; it can't disambiguate same-day PENDING twins. Fix: the dedupe now appends an OCCURRENCE INDEX counted PER FILE (contentKey#0, #1, ...). Two identical line items in ONE statement are two real transactions (#0, #1) and both survive; the SAME row across two overlapping files (each its own #0) still dedupes against the shared seen-set seeded #0..#(N-1) from the ledger; a genuine later Nth identical deposit lands on a fresh #index and is kept. Idempotent (re-import = 0 new). Proven-to-catch tests added: pending twins both kept; re-import no-op; overlapping files dedupe to one; a genuine third deposit kept. FULL end-to-end validation on the real file now PASSES: 1520 source -> 1520 kept (0 dropped; In rose exactly +$200 = the recovered deposit, to $152,719.41), all 1520 ids unique, 121 distinct posting dates (no collapse — April 261 rows/22 days, was showing "290 all Apr 30"), auditBalanceContinuity ok (opening $300.50 -> closing $1,007.88), re-import a clean 0-new no-op. bulk-statement-import (18) + imported-view (38) + statement-import (20) = 76 passed; lint + quality-manifest green.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/bulk-statement-import.js

### REV-0104 · Upgrade (family books) — per-account "reconciles / check register" badge: the account-of-record proof, in the app
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/imported-view.js (reconcileAccounts) · app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (accountReconciliation useMemo + prop) · app/src/components/BooksAccounts.jsx (per-account badge)
- **Type:** ui-ux
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Darrell asked: "Does Christina identify each Asset and their money flow... are they in their correct account of record?" The app already models each asset — Books > Accounts defines every account with name/institution/type (checking/savings/credit)/last-4 fragment/owning entity/opening balance, and money flow is filed per accountId with balances derived live (deriveAccountBalances, DR-0076). What was missing was the PROOF that each transaction sits in its correct account of record. Built it from the REV-0101 continuity engine: reconcileAccounts() audits EACH account's rows independently — a misfiled transaction breaks that account's bank-balance chain, so a green per-account result means those rows genuinely belong there. Surfaced in the app (APP-IS-PRIMARY, DR-0104 review-as-user): each account row now shows "reconciles" (green — register complete, un-double-counted, all rows belong here; tooltip names the opening->closing) or "check register" (amber — N balance breaks; a row may be misfiled to another account, missing, or duplicated; reset + re-import). Truthful-or-absent: no badge unless the account's rows actually carry bank balances to audit. Answer to Darrell, verified on Christina's real file: her checking statement reconciled ($300.50 -> $1,007.88, 1502 rows, one chain) — which PROVES those rows all belong to that one account of record. Note on routing: single-file import files to the account the user explicitly picks (correct by choice); bulk auto-routes by the filename's digits -> account fragment, so a file whose name lacks the last-4 (e.g. "Chase_01.01.26_07.17.26.xlsx") should use the single-file pick. reconcileAccounts tests: audits each account independently + flags the non-reconciling one; ignores accountId-less rows; {} for empty. imported-view 40 passed; books-accounts-render green; monolith guard holds (5447->5449, +2 shell wiring for the extracted helper, stated reason logged); lint + build green.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/imported-view.js

### REV-0106 · Fix (family taxes, WRONG-GUIDANCE) — 1099-NEC threshold now honors the tax YEAR; entity type no longer silently flattened on sync
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/components/Contractors1099.jsx (tax-year selector + threaded year) · app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (currentDate prop) · infra/supabase/migrations-auto/0106-entity-type-widen.sql · app/src/lib/entities-sync.js (normalizeEntityType)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Darrell: "Review all of them [the tax surfaces] for qualitative and quantitative methods... make sure it works when Christina uploads... interconnectedness... alignment with law, no promises." Three parallel reviewers audited 1099s / Entities / Calendar; I audited the import path + cross-surface wiring + legal posture. Two WRONG-DATA defects fixed here (the ones that corrupt real tax data): (1) WRONG-GUIDANCE — the 1099 cards called classificationAdvisory()/necThresholdLabel() with NO year, so they always used the library default (2026 / $2,000 under the OBBBA). During the 2026 filing season Christina is filing TY2025, where the threshold is $600 — so a contractor paid $900 in 2025 wrongly read "under threshold, no 1099 needed" when a 1099-NEC was legally required. Fixed: a Tax Year selector (defaults to currentDate's year, 2024-2026 selectable) threaded through both calls at all four sites; proven-to-catch render test — a $900 contractor reads CROSSED at TY2025 ($600) and NOT crossed at TY2026 ($2,000). (2) SILENT DATA LOSS — entities-sync flattened entity_type to 'personal'|'business' (DB CHECK only allowed those two), so nonprofit/trust/joint/other set by the family were silently lost on the next sync. Fixed: migration 0106 widens the CHECK to the six types the UI offers (widening never rejects existing rows; idempotent DROP+ADD), and normalizeEntityType passes the real type through (unknown -> personal so an insert can never break). Proven-to-catch: all six survive, the four formerly-flattened are asserted NOT to become personal. The legal layer itself (worker-classification.js) is strong and current — NEC_THRESHOLD_BY_YEAR {2024:600,2025:600,2026:2000} sourced to the OBBBA, household/clergy/family/church/accountant advisories all carry "confirm with a CPA" (no promises, DR-0100), isolation-by-default access. Verified: contractors-1099-render + entities-sync-type + consistency-guard + worker-classification + entity-pollution = 47 passed; lint + build green; monolith budget holds 5449. Remaining review findings (tax-identity/EIN fields — a SENSITIVE-DATA decision to surface NOT build blind; contractor bulk-upload; ledger-derived YTD [interconnectedness]; calendar blank-date-vanish + missing quarterly-estimate seed dates + recurring-obligation reminders) are ranked and reported to Darrell for the next pass.

### REV-0107 · Build (family taxes, SOVEREIGN) — contractor tax-identity: full SSN/EIN stays on-device + NAS, cloud stores only last-4
- **Date:** 2026-07-18

Darrell set the architecture: "Only saves to the cellphone or the backup NAS devices... we store no [sensitive] data." Built the SOVEREIGN split: the cloud (contractors_1099, migration 0107, additive + RLS-inherited) stores only the NON-SECRET tracking fields — legal_name, mailing_address, tax_id_type (ein/ssn), tax_id_last4, w9_on_file — with a DB CHECK that tax_id_last4 can never exceed 4 chars (defense-in-depth so a full SSN can't smuggle through). The FULL taxpayer id lives ONLY on the device that entered it, in a new on-device vault (tax-id-vault.js, localStorage; exportForBackup/importFromBackup for the family's device->NAS backup, never cloud). The form captures the full id into a TRANSIENT taxIdFull field that is destructured OFF before the record is built, so only the last-4 ever reaches the record/sync. Proven-to-catch (the guarantee): the synced payload is asserted NOT to contain the full id (only last-4 + type + W-9 flag); the vault round-trips device->NAS->device; masked display shows type + ····last4 only; a full id mistakenly placed in the last-4 field is truncated to 4. Row shows the masked ID + a "W-9 NEEDED to file" flag when the NEC threshold is crossed but no W-9 is on file. Opportunities/constraints stated to Darrell (zero cloud-SSN breach surface + sovereignty vs device-bound full id + NAS backup essential + app tracks-not-e-files). Verified: tax-id-vault (9) + contractors-1099-render (6) + consistency-guard (13) = 28 passed; lint + build green; monolith budget holds 5449. NAS backup runbook (ConnectBot) to follow as the device->NAS step.

### REV-0109 · Fix (family books, CRITICAL) — the register showed the account's SINGLE current balance on every row; now a per-row running balance
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/imported-view.js (runningBalanceByTxId) · app/src/components/BooksTransactions.jsx (per-row balance display + help copy)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Christina (with a screenshot): every transaction row read the SAME balance — "(now -$3,319)" repeated on all of Chase Personal Checking 7206's rows — instead of the balance as of each date. Reality-traced: BooksTransactions renderRow used `currentBal = balanceByAccount[acc.id]` (the account's ONE derived current balance, identical for every row, line ~886) and rendered "(now $X)" on each line, ignoring the bank's per-row balance we now import (t.balance, REV-0101/0105). Fix: new pure helper runningBalanceByTxId(transactions, accounts) returns the balance AS OF each transaction, keyed by tx id — per account, oldest-first, using the bank's OWN imported balance per row when present (authoritative) and chaining the rest off the opening balance so a manual row between two bank rows still computes. The row now shows "(bal $Y)" — the statement-style running balance that changes date to date — and falls back to the account's current balance "(now $X)" only for manual rows with no per-row figure (truthful-or-absent). Help copy updated to match. Proven-to-catch: bank balances used per row and are distinct (Set size 3, not 1 — the exact "same number on every row" bug); no-balance rows compute a running balance from opening; a manual row chains off the last bank balance; float-safe. Verified: imported-view (44) + books-tx-white-screen (20) + consistency-guard (13) passed; full-src lint + build green.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/imported-view.js

Unsure which guards a change touches -> run the full vitest. Green feature tests are NOT a green build. Recorded as inherited memory feedback_run_full_guard_suite_before_push. Pairs with DR-0076 (prove it — a green CI IS the evidence) + DR-0103 (the gate is the brake; don't waste its cycles). No code change; a verification-discipline WAY.

### REV-0114 · Fix (family books, CRITICAL) — import commit silently dropped rows on burst; add upload retry so transient failures survive
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/upload-retry.js (new) · app/src/lib/table-sync.js (upload wraps INSERT in withUploadRetry)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Christina (correctly): "validate the system, don't assume the CSV is incomplete — every discrepancy so far has been an app issue." Validated: her EXACT Chase file through the app's real modules (statement-import parse -> planBulkImport -> imported-view groupByMonth/totals/auditBalanceContinuity) yields June = 323 tx, in $29,751 out $30,356, reconciles $300.50 -> $1,007.88, 0 rows dropped — the app interprets the source CORRECTLY. So the live app showing June = 166 is a STORED-data discrepancy, not a CSV or display-logic problem. Root cause found in the commit path: addTransaction (poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx:3113) fires transactionsSync.upload PER ROW, fire-and-forget, and table-sync.upload had NO retry — a reset-and-re-import fires hundreds of INSERTs in a burst, so any row hitting a transient error (429 rate-limit / network blip / timeout) was caught-and-warned and left LOCAL-ONLY, never reaching the cloud. The Imported view reads the cloud (the NAS Python job over the synced DB), so the highest-volume month (June, 323) lost the most — matching the symptom precisely. Fix: new upload-retry.js — classifyUploadError (permanent: unique/RLS/FK/validation, which a retry can't fix and must NOT retry so a re-import can't double-write; transient: everything else) + withUploadRetry (backoff 250/500/1000/cap 2000, injected sleep, never throws). table-sync.upload now wraps its INSERT so a burst no longer silently drops rows. Proven-to-catch: transient failure retries until it lands (calls==3, row saved); permanent unique_violation stops at 1 call (no double-write); gives up after N with the failure; a thrown insert is caught + retried. Verified: upload-retry (8) + ALL 30 sync tests + quality-manifest + consistency + completion = 318 passed; monolith 5432, legibility REGRESSION 0, full-src lint + build green. NOTE (honest, DR-0076): this hardens the write path and is unit-proven, but confirming it resolves Christina's SPECIFIC June gap needs a real import on her instance (the cloud path can't be exercised from the sandbox) — a post-import "N of M landed" reconciliation readout is the next step so the gap becomes visible + self-healing.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/upload-retry.js

### REV-0115 · Build (family books) — the "N of M saved" import reconciliation readout: imports verify their own cloud writes + self-heal
- **Date:** 2026-07-18
- **Surface:** app/src/lib/import-commit.js (new) · app/src/poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx (commitImportedRows) · app/src/components/BooksTransactions.jsx (importCsv/commitBulk/resetAndImport use it)
- **Type:** correctness
- **Status:** logged
- **Findings:** Closes the loop on Christina's books after REV-0114 (upload retry): imports used to fire per-row cloud uploads fire-and-forget, so a partial save was SILENT (the June gap). Now the import AWAITS every upload, counts what actually landed, RE-UPLOADS the misses, and reports "Saved N of M to the cloud" — a partial save is visible + self-healing, never silent again (EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, DR-0076). New import-commit.js (pure, injected uploader): reconcileCommit (saved/failed/failedRows/remoteIds/offline — offline = sync off, local-only, not a failure so it doesn't cry wolf), commitRowsToCloud (awaits all, a thrown upload becomes a failed row, never propagates), commitWithRepair (re-uploads the misses up to N passes — the self-heal; does not thrash when offline), commitReadout (the one-line message: "Saved all N" / "Saved N of M — K did not upload, re-run; already-saved rows won't double up" / "Saved locally — sign in to sync"). Shell adds commitImportedRows (add all rows locally once, then commitWithRepair via transactionsSync.upload, fold remoteIds back); the three import handlers (importCsv, commitBulk, resetAndImport) now await it and alert the readout instead of a silent forEach. Proven-to-catch: partial commit reports the exact gap + returns the failed rows; a row that misses pass 1 lands on the repair pass (saved goes 2->3); rows that still fail are reported not hidden; offline does one pass only; readout copy for all/partial/offline/empty. Verified: import-commit (10) + upload-retry (8) + books-tx-white-screen + bulk-statement-import + consistency + quality-manifest + completion = 78 passed; monolith 5445 (under 5449; persistent-share regenerated), legibility REGRESSION 0, full-src lint + build green. Now the NEXT import on Christina's instance will TELL us if any rows fail to upload — turning the silent June gap into a measured, self-healing number.
- **Source:** app/src/lib/import-commit.js
